   189→class _DynamicPathProxy:
   190→    """Path-like proxy that resolves against runtime-root-aware accessors."""
   191→
   192→    def __init__(self, resolver: Callable[[], Path], *, label: str) -> None:
   193→        self._resolver = resolver
   194→        self._label = label
   195→
   196→    def _value(self) -> Path:
   197→        return self._resolver()
   198→
   199→    def __fspath__(self) -> str:
   200→        return str(self._value())
   201→
   202→    def __str__(self) -> str:
   203→        return str(self._value())
   204→
   205→    def __repr__(self) -> str:
   206→        return f"{self._label}({self._value()!s})"
   207→
   208→    def __truediv__(self, other: object) -> Path:
   209→        return self._value() / other
   210→
   211→    def __rtruediv__(self, other: object) -> Path:
   212→        return Path(other) / self._value()
   213→
   214→    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> object:
   215→        return getattr(self._value(), name)
   216→
   217→
   218→def get_state_dir() -> Path:
   219→    """Return state directory anchored at the active runtime project root."""
   220→    return get_project_root() / ".desloppify"
   221→
   222→
   223→def get_state_file() -> Path:
   224→    """Return default state file anchored at the active runtime project root."""
   225→    return get_state_dir() / "state.json"
   226→
   227→
   228→# Legacy exports retained for import compatibility, now runtime-root-aware.

   230→STATE_FILE = _DynamicPathProxy(get_state_file, label="STATE_FILE")

   239→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   240→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   241→    return {

   243→        "created": utc_now(),
   244→        "last_scan": None,
   245→        "scan_count": 0,
   246→        "overall_score": 0,

   258→
   259→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   260→    try:
   261→        parsed = int(value)
   262→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   263→        return default
   264→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   265→
   266→
   267→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   268→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   269→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   270→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   271→
   272→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   273→        state["findings"] = {}
   274→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   275→        state["stats"] = {}
   276→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   277→        state["scan_history"] = []
   278→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   279→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   280→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   281→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   282→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   283→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   284→
   285→    findings = state["findings"]
   286→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   287→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   288→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   289→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   290→            continue
   291→
   292→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
   293→        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   294→        finding.setdefault("file", "")
   295→        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
   296→        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   297→        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
   298→        finding.setdefault("detail", {})
   299→        finding.setdefault("status", "open")
   300→        finding["status"] = canonical_finding_status(
   301→            finding.get("status"),
   302→            default="open",
   303→        )
   304→        finding.setdefault("note", None)
   305→        finding.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   306→        finding.setdefault("last_seen", finding["first_seen"])
   307→        finding.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   308→        finding["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   309→            finding.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   310→        )
   311→        finding.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   312→        finding.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   313→        finding.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   314→
   315→    for finding_id in to_remove:
   316→        findings.pop(finding_id, None)
   317→
   318→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   319→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   320→            continue
   321→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   322→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   323→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   324→
   325→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   326→    return None
   327→
   328→
   329→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   330→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   331→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   332→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   333→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   334→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   335→
   336→    findings = state["findings"]
   337→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   338→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   339→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
   340→        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
   341→            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
   342→        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
   343→            raise ValueError(
   344→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
   345→            )
   346→
   347→        tier = finding.get("tier")
   348→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   349→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   350→
   351→        reopen_count = finding.get("reopen_count")
   352→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   353→            raise ValueError(
   354→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
   355→            )
   356→
   357→
   358→def require_findings(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Finding]:
   359→    """Return ``state['findings']`` and fail fast when contract is violated."""
   360→    findings = state.get("findings")
   361→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   362→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   363→    return findings
   364→
   365→
   366→def require_subjective_assessments(
   367→    state: StateModel | dict[str, Any],
   368→) -> dict[str, SubjectiveAssessment]:
   369→    """Return ``state['subjective_assessments']`` with strict contract checks."""
   370→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   371→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict):
   372→        raise ValueError("state.subjective_assessments must be a dict")
   373→    return assessments
   374→
   375→
   376→def require_scan_count(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> int:
   377→    """Return normalized ``state['scan_count']`` and fail on missing/invalid values."""
   378→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count")
   379→    if not isinstance(scan_count, int) or scan_count < 0:
   380→        raise ValueError("state.scan_count must be a non-negative integer")
   381→    return scan_count
   382→
   383→
   384→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
   385→    """JSON serializer that handles known types and rejects unknowns."""
   386→    if isinstance(obj, set):
   387→        return sorted(obj)
   388→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
   389→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
   390→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
   391→        return obj.isoformat()
   392→    raise TypeError(
   393→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
   394→    )
   395→
   396→
   397→def get_overall_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   398→    """Canonical overall score (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   399→    return state.get("overall_score")
   400→

   405→

   393→    ) -> None:
   394→        if event == "heartbeat":
   395→            active = details.get("active_batches")
   396→            queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   397→            elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   398→            if not isinstance(active, list):
   399→                active = []
   400→            if not isinstance(queued, list):

   402→            if not active and not queued:

   404→            segments: list[str] = []

   410→                segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   411→            if len(active) > 6:
   412→                segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   413→            queued_segment = ""

   416→            print(

   424→            self.append_run_log(

   427→                f"elapsed={{{', '.join(f'{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}' for idx in active)}}}"

   429→            if self.stall_warning_seconds > 0 and isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   430→                slow_active = [

   433→                    if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   434→                    and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= self.stall_warning_seconds
   435→                ]
   436→                newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in self.stall_warned_batches]
   437→                if newly_warned:
   438→                    self.stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   439→                    warning_message = (

   442→                        f"{self.stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "

   444→                    )

   446→                    self.append_run_log(

   449→                        f"batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   450→                    )
   451→            return

   499→            state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   500→            state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")

   503→            print(

990→def _execute_batch_run(coordinator: ReviewBatchRunCoordinator) -> None:
   991→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   992→    args = coordinator.args
   993→    state = coordinator.state
   994→    lang = coordinator.lang
   995→    state_file = coordinator.state_file
   996→    deps = coordinator.deps
   997→    config = coordinator.config
   998→    config = config or {}
   999→    non_fatal_warnings: list[NonFatalIssue] = []
  1000→    settings = _resolve_batch_run_settings(args, colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn)
  1001→    runner = settings.runner
  1002→    allow_partial = settings.allow_partial
  1003→    run_parallel = settings.run_parallel
  1004→    max_parallel_batches = settings.max_parallel_batches
  1005→    heartbeat_seconds = settings.heartbeat_seconds
  1006→    batch_timeout_seconds = settings.batch_timeout_seconds
  1007→    batch_max_retries = settings.batch_max_retries
  1008→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = settings.batch_retry_backoff_seconds
  1009→    stall_warning_seconds = settings.stall_warning_seconds
  1010→    stall_kill_seconds = settings.stall_kill_seconds
  1011→
  1012→    stamp = runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp()
  1013→    packet, immutable_packet_path, prompt_packet_path = deps.load_or_prepare_packet_fn(
  1014→        args,
  1015→        state=state,
  1016→        lang=lang,
  1017→        config=config,
  1018→        stamp=stamp,
  1019→    )
  1020→
  1021→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
  1022→    packet_dimensions = _normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
  1023→    scored_dimensions = _scored_dimensions_for_lang(
  1024→        lang.name,
  1025→        warning_sink=non_fatal_warnings,
  1026→    )
  1027→    summarize_non_fatal_issues(
  1028→        non_fatal_warnings,
  1029→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
  1030→    )
  1031→    _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
  1032→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
  1033→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
  1034→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
  1035→        scan_path=scan_path,
  1036→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
  1037→    )
  1038→    suggested_prepare_cmd = f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
  1039→    batches = _require_batches(
  1040→        packet,
  1041→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
  1042→        suggested_prepare_cmd=suggested_prepare_cmd,
  1043→    )
  1044→
  1045→    selected_indexes = runner_helpers_mod.selected_batch_indexes(
  1046→        raw_selection=getattr(args, "only_batches", None),
  1047→        batch_count=len(batches),
  1048→        parse_fn=batch_core_mod.parse_batch_selection,
  1049→        colorize_fn=deps.colorize_fn,
  1050→    )
  1051→    total_batches = len(selected_indexes)
  1052→    effective_workers = min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches) if run_parallel else 1
  1053→    waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
  1054→    worst_case_seconds = waves * batch_timeout_seconds
  1055→    worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil(worst_case_seconds / 60))
  1056→    print(
  1057→        deps.colorize_fn(
  1058→            "  Runtime expectation: "
  1059→            f"{total_batches} batch(es), workers={effective_workers}, "
  1060→            f"timeout-per-batch={int(batch_timeout_seconds / 60)}m, "
  1061→            f"worst-case upper bound ~{worst_case_minutes}m.",
  1062→            "dim",

666→def _run_batch_execution_stage(
   667→    *,
   668→    packet: dict[str, object],
   669→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   670→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   671→    log_files: dict[int, Path],

   673→    tracker: _BatchExecutionTracker,

   675→    settings: _BatchRunSettings,
   676→    immutable_packet_path: Path,
   677→    logs_dir: Path,
   678→) -> _BatchExecutionOutcome:
   679→    """Stage 3: run tasks, collect normalized results, and enforce failure policy."""
   680→    tasks = _build_batch_tasks(
   681→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   682→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   683→        output_files=output_files,

   694→                f"heartbeat={settings.heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   695→                "dim",
   696→            )
   697→        )
   698→
   699→    try:
   700→        execution_failures = runner_helpers_mod.execute_batches(
   701→            tasks=tasks,
   702→            run_parallel=settings.run_parallel,
   703→            progress_fn=tracker.report_progress,
   704→            error_log_fn=tracker.record_execution_issue,
   705→            max_parallel_workers=settings.max_parallel_batches,
   706→            heartbeat_seconds=settings.heartbeat_seconds,
   707→        )
   708→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   709→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   710→            key = str(idx + 1)
   711→            state = tracker.batch_status.setdefault(
   712→                key,
   713→                {"position": tracker.batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   714→            )
   715→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   716→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   717→        tracker.write_run_summary(
   718→            successful_batches=[],
   719→            failed_batches=[],
   720→            interrupted=True,
   721→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   722→        )
   723→        tracker.append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   724→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   725→
   726→    allowed_dims = {
   727→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   728→    }
   729→    batch_results, failures = runner_helpers_mod.collect_batch_results(
   730→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   731→        failures=execution_failures,
   732→        output_files=output_files,
   733→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   734→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(
   735→            raw,
   736→            log_fn=log,
   737→        ),
   738→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   739→            payload,
   740→            dims,
   741→            max_batch_findings=max_batch_findings_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   742→            abstraction_sub_axes=deps.abstraction_sub_axes,
   743→        ),
   744→    )
   745→
   746→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   747→    failure_set = set(failures)
   748→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   749→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   750→        key = str(idx + 1)
   751→        state = tracker.batch_status.setdefault(
   752→            key,
   753→            {"position": tracker.batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   754→        )
   755→        if idx not in failure_set:
   756→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   757→            continue
   758→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   759→            state["status"] = "failed"
   760→            continue
   761→        if not output_files[idx].exists():

   797→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   798→        )

   847→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   848→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   849→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   850→    if reviewed_files:
   851→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   852→        print(

   855→                "dim",

   861→        run_stamp=stamp,

   863→    )
   864→    merged_assessment_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(

   866→    )
   867→    merged_finding_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(

1→"""Concern generators — mechanical findings → subjective review bridge.
     2→
     3→Concerns are ephemeral: computed on-demand from current state, never persisted.
     4→Only LLM-confirmed concerns become persistent Finding objects via review import.
     5→
     6→Generators focus on cross-cutting synthesis — bundling all signals per file so
     7→the LLM gets a complete picture, and surfacing systemic patterns across files
     8→that no single detector captures.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from collections import defaultdict
    15→from dataclasses import dataclass
    16→from typing import Any, Literal, TypedDict, cast
    17→
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class Concern:
    23→    """A potential design problem surfaced by mechanical signals."""
    24→
    25→    type: str  # concern classification
    26→    file: str  # primary file (relative path)
    27→    summary: str  # human-readable 1-liner
    28→    evidence: tuple[str, ...]  # supporting data points
    29→    question: str  # specific question for LLM to evaluate
    30→    fingerprint: str  # stable hash for dismissal tracking
    31→    source_findings: tuple[str, ...]  # finding IDs that triggered this
    32→
    33→
    34→class ConcernSignals(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    """Typed signal payload extracted from mechanical findings."""
    36→
    37→    max_params: float
    38→    max_nesting: float
    39→    loc: float
    40→    function_count: float
    41→    monster_loc: float
    42→    monster_funcs: list[str]
    43→
    44→
    45→_NUMERIC_SIGNAL_KEYS = ("max_params", "max_nesting", "loc", "function_count")
    46→SignalKey = Literal["max_params", "max_nesting", "loc", "function_count", "monster_loc"]
    47→
    48→
    49→def _update_max_signal(signals: ConcernSignals, key: SignalKey, value: object) -> None:
    50→    """Update numeric signal key with max(existing, value) when value is valid."""
    51→    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int | float) or value <= 0:
    52→        return
    53→    current = float(signals.get(key, 0.0))
    54→    signals[key] = max(current, float(value))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _fingerprint(concern_type: str, file: str, key_signals: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
    58→    """Stable hash of (type, file, sorted key signals)."""
    59→    raw = f"{concern_type}::{file}::{','.join(sorted(key_signals))}"
    60→    return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
    61→
    62→
    63→def _is_dismissed(
    64→    dismissals: dict, fingerprint: str, source_finding_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    65→) -> bool:
    66→    """Check if a concern was previously dismissed and source findings unchanged."""
    67→    entry = dismissals.get(fingerprint)
    68→    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    69→        return False
    70→    prev_sources = set(entry.get("source_finding_ids", []))
    71→    return prev_sources == set(source_finding_ids)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _open_findings(state: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    75→    """Return all open findings from state."""
    76→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    77→    return [
    78→        f for f in findings.values()
    79→        if isinstance(f, dict) and f.get("status") == "open"
    80→    ]
    81→
    82→
    83→def _group_by_file(state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    84→    """Group open findings by file, excluding holistic (file='.')."""
    85→    by_file: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
    86→    for f in _open_findings(state):
    87→        file = f.get("file", "")
    88→        if file and file != ".":
    89→            by_file[file].append(f)
    90→    return dict(by_file)
    91→
    92→
    93→# ── Signal extraction ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    94→
    95→
    96→def _extract_signals(findings: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> ConcernSignals:
    97→    """Extract key quantitative signals from a file's findings."""
    98→    signals: ConcernSignals = {}
    99→    monster_funcs: list[str] = []
   100→

    20→def _try_reconfigure_stream(stream: object) -> bool:
    21→    """Attempt line-buffered reconfigure on a stream, returning success."""
    22→    reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    23→    if not callable(reconfigure):
    24→        return False
    25→    try:
    26→        reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    27→    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→        return False
    29→    return True
    30→
    31→
    32→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    33→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    34→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    35→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    36→        _try_reconfigure_stream(stream)
    37→
    38→
    39→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    40→    """Prepare or import subjective code review findings."""
    41→    _enable_live_review_output()
    42→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    43→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    44→    state = runtime.state
    45→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    46→

    50→            file=sys.stderr,
    51→        )
    52→        sys.exit(1)
    53→
    54→    merge = bool(getattr(args, "merge", False))
    55→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))
    56→    external_start = bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False))
    57→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
    58→    import_file = getattr(args, "import_file", None)
    59→    validate_import_file = getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None)
    60→
    61→    import_mode = bool(import_file) and not external_submit
    62→    mode_flags = [
    63→        merge,
    64→        run_batches,
    65→        external_start,
    66→        external_submit,
    67→        import_mode,
    68→        bool(validate_import_file),
    69→    ]
    70→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:

    66→    max_age_days: int = 30
    67→    force_refresh: bool = True
    68→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    69→    config_dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    70→    files: list[str] | None = None
    71→
    72→
    73→@dataclass
    74→class HolisticReviewPrepareOptions:
    75→    """Configuration bundle for holistic review preparation."""
    76→
    77→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    78→    files: list[str] | None = None
    79→    include_full_sweep: bool = True
    80→    max_files_per_batch: int | None = None
    81→    include_issue_history: bool = False
    82→    issue_history_max_issues: int = 30
    83→    issue_history_max_batch_items: int = 20
    84→
    85→def _rel_list(s) -> list[str]:
    86→    """Normalize a set or list of paths to sorted relative paths (max 10)."""
    87→    if isinstance(s, set):
    88→        return sorted(rel(x) for x in s)[:10]
    89→    return [rel(x) for x in list(s)[:10]]
    90→
    91→
    92→def _normalize_max_files(value: Any) -> int | None:
    93→    """Normalize max_files input: None/<=0 means unlimited."""
    94→    if value in (None, ""):
    95→        return None
    96→    parsed = int(value)
    97→    return parsed if parsed > 0 else None
    98→
    99→
   100→def _relative_to_base(filepath: str, *, resolved_base: Path) -> str | None:
   101→    """Return ``filepath`` relative to ``resolved_base`` when inside the base tree."""
   102→    try:
   103→        resolved = Path(filepath).resolve()
   104→        return resolved.relative_to(resolved_base).as_posix()
   105→    except ValueError:
   106→        return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def _collect_allowed_review_files(
   110→    files: list[str],
   111→    lang: object,
   112→    *,
   113→    base_path: Path | None = None,
   114→) -> set[str]:
   115→    """Return relative production-file paths allowed for holistic review batches."""
   116→    allowed: set[str] = set()
   117→    zone_map = getattr(lang, "zone_map", None)
   118→    resolved_base = base_path.resolve() if isinstance(base_path, Path) else None
   119→    for filepath in files:
   120→        if not isinstance(filepath, str):
   121→            continue
   122→        normalized = filepath.strip().replace("\\", "/")
   123→        if not normalized:
   124→            continue
   125→        if zone_map is not None:
   126→            try:
   127→                zone = zone_map.get(filepath)
   128→                zone_value = getattr(zone, "value", str(zone))
   129→            except (AttributeError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
   130→                zone_value = "production"
   131→            if zone_value in _NON_PRODUCTION_ZONES:
   132→                continue
   133→        allowed.add(normalized)
   134→        allowed.add(rel(filepath))
   135→        if resolved_base is not None:
   136→            relative = _relative_to_base(filepath, resolved_base=resolved_base)
   137→            if relative is not None:
   138→                allowed.add(relative)
   139→    return allowed
   140→
   141→
   142→def _file_in_allowed_scope(filepath: object, allowed_files: set[str]) -> bool:
   143→    """True when *filepath* resolves to a currently in-scope review file."""
   144→    if not isinstance(filepath, str):
   145→        return False
   146→    normalized = filepath.strip().replace("\\", "/")
   147→    if not normalized:
   148→        return False
   149→    if normalized in allowed_files:
   150→        return True
   151→    return rel(filepath) in allowed_files
   152→
   153→
   154→def _filter_issue_focus_to_scope(
   155→    issue_focus: object,
   156→    allowed_files: set[str],
   157→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   158→    """Drop out-of-scope related_files from historical issue focus payload."""
   159→    if not isinstance(issue_focus, dict):
   160→        return None
   161→    issues_raw = issue_focus.get("issues", [])
   162→    issues: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   163→    if isinstance(issues_raw, list):
   164→        for raw_issue in issues_raw:
   165→            if not isinstance(raw_issue, dict):
   166→                continue
   167→            issue = dict(raw_issue)
   168→            related_raw = issue.get("related_files", [])
   169→            if isinstance(related_raw, list):
   170→                issue["related_files"] = [
   171→                    path
   172→                    for path in related_raw
   173→                    if _file_in_allowed_scope(path, allowed_files)
   174→                ]
   175→            issues.append(issue)
   176→    scoped = dict(issue_focus)
   177→    scoped["issues"] = issues
   178→    scoped["selected_count"] = len(issues)
   179→    return scoped
   180→
   181→
   182→def _filter_batches_to_file_scope(
   183→    batches: list[dict[str, Any]],
   184→    *,
   185→    allowed_files: set[str],
   186→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   187→    """Strip out-of-scope files/signals from review batches."""
   188→    if not allowed_files:
   189→        return []
   190→
   191→    scoped_batches: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   192→    for raw_batch in batches:
   193→        if not isinstance(raw_batch, dict):
   194→            continue
   195→        batch = dict(raw_batch)
   196→        files_to_read = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   197→        normalized_files_to_read: list[Any] = []
   198→        if isinstance(files_to_read, list):
   199→            normalized_files_to_read = [
   200→                filepath
   201→                for filepath in files_to_read
   202→                if _file_in_allowed_scope(filepath, allowed_files)
   203→            ]
   204→        batch["files_to_read"] = normalized_files_to_read
   205→
   206→        concern_signals = batch.get("concern_signals", [])
   207→        if isinstance(concern_signals, list):
   208→            batch["concern_signals"] = [
   209→                signal
   210→                for signal in concern_signals
   211→                if isinstance(signal, dict)
   212→                and _file_in_allowed_scope(signal.get("file", ""), allowed_files)
   213→            ]
   214→            if "concern_signal_count" in batch:
   215→                batch["concern_signal_count"] = len(batch["concern_signals"])
   216→
   217→        issue_focus = _filter_issue_focus_to_scope(
   218→            batch.get("historical_issue_focus"),
   219→            allowed_files,
   220→        )
   221→        if issue_focus is not None:
   222→            batch["historical_issue_focus"] = issue_focus
   223→
   224→        has_seed_files = bool(batch["files_to_read"])
   225→        has_signals = bool(batch.get("concern_signals"))
   226→        if has_seed_files or has_signals:
   227→            scoped_batches.append(batch)
   228→    return scoped_batches
   229→
   230→
   231→def prepare_review(
   232→    path: Path,
   233→    lang: object,
   234→    state: dict,
   235→    options: ReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   236→) -> dict[str, object]:
   237→    """Prepare review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict.
   238→
   239→    If *files* is provided, skip file_finder (avoids redundant filesystem walks
   240→    when the caller already has the file list, e.g. from _setup_lang).
   241→    """
   242→    resolved_options = options or ReviewPrepareOptions()
   243→    resolved_options.max_files = _normalize_max_files(resolved_options.max_files)
   244→    all_files = (
   245→        resolved_options.files
   246→        if resolved_options.files is not None
   247→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
   248→    )
   249→
   250→    # Enable file cache for entire prepare operation — context building,
   251→    # file selection, and content extraction all read the same files.
   252→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
   253→    if not already_cached:
   254→        enable_file_cache()
   255→    try:
   256→        context = build_review_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   257→        selected = select_files_for_review(
   258→            lang,
   259→            path,
   260→            state,
   261→            options=ReviewSelectionOptions(
   262→                max_files=resolved_options.max_files,
   263→                max_age_days=resolved_options.max_age_days,
   264→                force_refresh=resolved_options.force_refresh,
   265→                files=all_files,
   266→            ),
   267→        )
   268→        file_requests = _build_file_requests(selected, lang, state)
   269→    finally:
   270→        if not already_cached:
   271→            disable_file_cache()
   272→
   273→    default_dims, dimension_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   274→    dims = resolve_dimensions(
   275→        cli_dimensions=resolved_options.dimensions,
   276→        config_dimensions=resolved_options.config_dimensions,
   277→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   278→    )
   279→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance(lang.name)
   280→    valid_dims = set(dimension_prompts)
   281→    invalid_requested = [
   282→        dim for dim in (resolved_options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   283→    ]
   284→    invalid_config = [
   285→        dim
   286→        for dim in (resolved_options.config_dimensions or [])
   287→        if dim not in valid_dims
   288→    ]
   289→
   290→    return {
   291→        "command": "review",
   292→        "language": lang.name,
   293→        "dimensions": dims,
   294→        "dimension_prompts": {
   295→            d: dimension_prompts[d] for d in dims if d in dimension_prompts
   296→        },
   297→        "lang_guidance": lang_guide,
   298→        "context": serialize_context(context),
   299→        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   300→        "files": file_requests,
   301→        "total_candidates": len(file_requests),
   302→        "cache_status": {
   303→            "fresh": count_fresh(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   304→            "stale": count_stale(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   305→            "new": len(file_requests),
   306→        },
   307→        "invalid_dimensions": {
   308→            "requested": invalid_requested,
   309→            "config": invalid_config,
   310→        },
   311→    }
   312→
   313→
   314→def _build_file_requests(files: list[str], lang, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   315→    """Build per-file review request dicts."""
   316→    file_requests = []
   317→    for filepath in files:
   318→        content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
   319→        if content is None:
   320→            continue
   321→
   322→        rpath = rel(filepath)
   323→        zone = "production"
   324→        if lang.zone_map is not None:
   325→            zone = lang.zone_map.get(filepath).value
   326→
   327→        neighbors: dict
   328→        if lang.dep_graph:
   329→            entry = dep_graph_lookup(lang.dep_graph, filepath)
   330→            imports_raw = entry.get("imports", set())
   331→            importers_raw = entry.get("importers", set())
   332→            importer_count_value = importer_count(entry)
   333→            neighbors = {
   334→                "imports": _rel_list(imports_raw),
   335→                "importers": _rel_list(importers_raw),
   336→                "importer_count": importer_count_value,
   337→            }
   338→        else:
   339→            neighbors = {}
   340→
   341→        file_requests.append(
   342→            {
   343→                "file": rpath,
   344→                "content": content,
   345→                "zone": zone,
   346→                "loc": len(content.splitlines()),
   347→                "neighbors": neighbors,
   348→                "existing_findings": get_file_findings(state, filepath),
   349→            }
   350→        )
   351→    return file_requests
   352→
   353→
   354→def prepare_holistic_review(
   355→    path: Path,
   356→    lang: object,
   357→    state: dict,
   358→    options: HolisticReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   359→) -> dict[str, object]:
   360→    """Prepare holistic review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict."""
   361→    resolved_options = options or HolisticReviewPrepareOptions()
   362→    all_files = (
   363→        resolved_options.files
   364→        if resolved_options.files is not None
   365→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
   366→    )
   367→    allowed_review_files = _collect_allowed_review_files(
   368→        all_files,
   369→        lang,
   370→        base_path=path,
   371→    )
   372→
   373→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
   374→    if not already_cached:
   375→        enable_file_cache()
   376→    try:
   377→        context = HolisticContext.from_raw(
   378→            build_holistic_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   379→        )
   380→        # Also include per-file review context for reference
   381→        review_ctx = build_review_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   382→    finally:
   383→        if not already_cached:
   384→            disable_file_cache()
   385→
   386→    default_dims, holistic_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   387→    _, per_file_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   388→    dims = resolve_dimensions(
   389→        cli_dimensions=resolved_options.dimensions,
   390→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   391→    )
   392→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance(lang.name)
   393→    valid_dims = set(holistic_prompts) | set(per_file_prompts)
   394→    invalid_requested = [
   395→        dim for dim in (resolved_options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   396→    ]
   397→    invalid_default = [dim for dim in default_dims if dim not in valid_dims]
   398→    batches = _build_investigation_batches(
   399→        context,
   400→        lang,
   401→        repo_root=path,
   402→        max_files_per_batch=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   403→    )
   404→
   405→    # Append design-coherence batch from mechanical concern signals.
   406→    concerns_batch = None
   407→    try:
   408→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang.name)
   409→        concerns = [
   410→            concern
   411→            for concern in concerns
   412→            if _file_in_allowed_scope(getattr(concern, "file", ""), allowed_review_files)
   413→        ]
   414→        concerns_batch = _batch_concerns(
   415→            concerns,
   416→            max_files=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   417→            active_dimensions=dims,
   418→        )
   419→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   420→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   421→        concerns_batch = None
   422→    if concerns_batch:
   423→        batches.append(concerns_batch)
   424→
   425→    batches = _filter_batches_to_dimensions(
   426→        batches,
   427→        dims,
   428→        fallback_max_files=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   429→    )
   430→    include_full_sweep = bool(resolved_options.include_full_sweep)
   431→    # Explicitly scoped dimension runs should stay scoped by default.
   432→    if resolved_options.dimensions:
   433→        include_full_sweep = False
   434→    if include_full_sweep:
   435→        append_full_sweep_batch(
   436→            batches=batches,
   437→            dims=dims,
   438→            all_files=all_files,
   439→            lang=lang,
   440→            max_files=resolved_options.max_files_per_batch,
   441→        )
   442→    batches = _filter_batches_to_file_scope(
   443→        batches,
   444→        allowed_files=allowed_review_files,
   445→    )
   446→
   447→    # Holistic mode can receive per-file-oriented dimensions via CLI suggestions.
   448→    # Attach whichever prompt definition exists so reviewers always get guidance.
   449→    selected_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   450→    for dim in dims:
   451→        prompt = holistic_prompts.get(dim)
   452→        if prompt is None:
   453→            prompt = per_file_prompts.get(dim)
   454→        if prompt is None:
   455→            continue
   456→        selected_prompts[dim] = prompt
   457→
   458→    payload = {
   459→        "command": "review",
   460→        "mode": "holistic",
   461→        "language": lang.name,
   462→        "dimensions": dims,
   463→        "dimension_prompts": selected_prompts,
   464→        "lang_guidance": lang_guide,
   465→        "holistic_context": context.to_dict(),
   466→        "review_context": serialize_context(review_ctx),
   467→        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   468→        "total_files": context.codebase_stats.get("total_files", 0),
   469→        "workflow": HOLISTIC_WORKFLOW,
   470→        "invalid_dimensions": {
   471→            "requested": invalid_requested,
   472→            "default": invalid_default,
   473→        },
   474→    }
   475→    if resolved_options.include_issue_history:
   476→        history_payload = build_issue_history_context(
   477→            state,
   478→            options=ReviewHistoryOptions(
   479→                max_issues=resolved_options.issue_history_max_issues,
   480→            ),
   481→        )
   482→        payload["historical_review_issues"] = history_payload
   483→        for batch in batches:
   484→            if not isinstance(batch, dict):
   485→                continue
   486→            batch_dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   487→            batch["historical_issue_focus"] = build_batch_issue_focus(
   488→                history_payload,
   489→                dimensions=batch_dims,
   490→                max_items=resolved_options.issue_history_max_batch_items,
   491→            )
   492→        batches = _filter_batches_to_file_scope(
   493→            batches,
   494→            allowed_files=allowed_review_files,
   495→        )
   496→    payload["investigation_batches"] = batches
   497→    return payload
   498→
```

   136→    }
   137→    valid_reviewed_files_abs = {
   138→        _absolutize_review_path(str(finding["file"]), project_root=resolved_project_root)
   139→        for finding in findings_list
   140→        if all(key in finding for key in required_fields)
   141→    }
   142→    valid_reviewed_files = valid_reviewed_files_rel | valid_reviewed_files_abs
   143→    reviewed_files_rel = {
   144→        str(file_path).strip()
   145→        for file_path in reviewed_files
   146→        if isinstance(file_path, str) and file_path.strip()
   147→    }
   148→    reviewed_files_abs = {
   149→        _absolutize_review_path(file_path, project_root=resolved_project_root)
   150→        for file_path in reviewed_files_rel
   151→    }
   152→    review_potential_files = valid_reviewed_files | {
   153→        *reviewed_files_rel,
   154→        *reviewed_files_abs,
   155→    }
   156→
   157→    potentials = _lang_potentials(state, lang_name)
   158→    potentials["review"] = len(review_potential_files)
   159→
   160→    diff = merge_scan(
   161→        state,
   162→        review_findings,
   163→        options=MergeScanOptions(
   164→            lang=lang_name,
   165→            potentials={"review": potentials.get("review", 0)},
   166→            merge_potentials=True,
   167→        ),
   168→    )
   169→
   170→    new_ids = {finding["id"] for finding in review_findings}
   171→    reimported_files = valid_reviewed_files
   172→    auto_resolve_review_findings(
   173→        state,
   174→        new_ids=new_ids,
   175→        diff=diff,
   176→        note="not reported in latest per-file re-import",
   177→        should_resolve=lambda finding: (
   178→            finding.get("detector") == "review"
   179→            and not finding.get("detail", {}).get("holistic")
   180→            and finding.get("file", "") in reimported_files
   181→        ),
   182→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   183→    )
   184→
   185→    if skipped:
   186→        diff["skipped"] = len(skipped)
   187→        diff["skipped_details"] = skipped
   188→
   189→    update_review_cache(
   190→        state,
   191→        findings_list,
   192→        reviewed_files=reviewed_files,
   193→        project_root=resolved_project_root,
   194→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   195→    )
   196→    return diff
   197→
   198→
   199→def update_review_cache(
   200→    state: dict[str, Any],
   201→    findings_data: list[ReviewFindingPayload],
   202→    *,
   203→    reviewed_files: list[str] | None = None,
   204→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
   205→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   206→) -> None:
   207→    """Update per-file review cache with timestamps and content hashes."""
   208→    findings_by_file: dict[str, int] = {}
   209→    for finding in findings_data:
   210→        file_path = finding.get("file")
   211→        if not isinstance(file_path, str):
   212→            continue
   213→        findings_by_file[file_path] = findings_by_file.get(file_path, 0) + 1
   214→
   215→    refresh_review_file_cache(
   216→        state,
   217→        reviewed_files=reviewed_files,
   218→        findings_by_file=findings_by_file,
   219→        project_root=project_root,
   220→        hash_file_fn=hash_file,
   221→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   222→    )
   223→
```

    70→def _state_subjective_assessments(

    82→def _state_lang_capabilities(

    86→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")

   160→def _record_scan_warning(

   162→    *,

   166→) -> None:

   169→    if not isinstance(warnings, list):
   170→        warnings = []

   172→    warnings.append(

   174→            "code": code,

   183→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   184→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   185→    config: dict[str, object],
   186→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   187→    lang: LangRun | None,
   188→) -> LangRun | None:
   189→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   190→    if not lang:
   191→        return None
   192→
   193→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   194→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   195→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   196→        lang,
   197→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   198→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   199→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   200→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   201→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   202→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   203→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   204→        ),
   205→    )
   206→
   207→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   208→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   209→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   210→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   211→    }
   212→    return runtime_lang
   213→
   214→
   215→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   216→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   217→    *,
   218→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   219→) -> int:
   220→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   221→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   222→
   223→    reset_keys = {
   224→        key.strip()
   225→        for key in assessments
   226→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   227→    }
   228→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   229→
   230→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   231→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   232→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   233→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   234→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   235→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   236→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   237→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   238→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   239→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   240→            payload.pop("components", None)
   241→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   242→            continue
   243→        assessments[key] = {
   244→            "score": 0.0,
   245→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   246→            "assessed_at": now,
   247→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   248→            "placeholder": True,
   249→        }
   250→    return len(reset_keys)
   251→
   252→
   253→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   254→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   255→) -> int:
   256→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   257→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   258→
   259→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   260→    expired = 0
   261→    for payload in assessments.values():
   262→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   263→            continue
   264→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   265→            continue
   266→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   267→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   268→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   269→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   270→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   271→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   272→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   273→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   274→        payload.pop("components", None)
   275→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   276→        expired += 1
   277→    return expired

   280→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   281→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   282→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   283→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   284→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   285→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   286→    path = Path(args.path)
   287→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   288→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   289→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   290→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   291→        state
   292→    )
   293→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   294→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   295→            state,
   296→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   297→        )
   298→
   299→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   300→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   301→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   302→
   303→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   304→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   305→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   306→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   307→
   308→    return ScanRuntime(
   309→        args=args,
   310→        state_path=state_file,
   311→        state=state,
   312→        project_root=get_project_root(),
   313→        path=path,
   314→        config=config,
   315→        lang=lang,
   316→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   317→        profile=profile,
   318→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   319→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   320→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   321→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   322→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   323→    )
   324→
   325→
   326→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   327→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   328→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   329→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   330→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   331→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   332→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   333→        return findings
   334→
   335→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   336→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   337→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, runtime.project_root
   338→    )
   339→    if not stale:
   340→        return findings
   341→
   342→    augmented = list(findings)
   343→    augmented.extend(stale)
   344→    for stale_finding in stale:
   345→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   346→    return augmented
   347→
   348→
   349→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   350→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   351→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   352→    *,
   353→    decay_scans: int,
   354→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   355→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   356→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   357→        findings,
   358→        state=runtime.state,
   359→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   360→        project_root=runtime.project_root,
   361→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   362→    )
   363→
   364→
   365→def run_scan_generation(
   366→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   367→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   368→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   369→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   370→        disable_parse_cache,
   371→        enable_parse_cache,
   372→        is_parse_cache_enabled,
   373→    )
   374→
   375→    file_cache_previously_enabled = is_file_cache_enabled()
   376→    parse_cache_previously_enabled = is_parse_cache_enabled()
   377→    if not file_cache_previously_enabled:
   378→        enable_file_cache()
   379→    if not parse_cache_previously_enabled:
   380→        enable_parse_cache()
   381→    try:
   382→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   383→            runtime.path,
   384→            lang=runtime.lang,
   385→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   386→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   387→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   388→                profile=runtime.profile,
   389→            ),
   390→        )
   391→    finally:
   392→        if not parse_cache_previously_enabled:
   393→            disable_parse_cache()
   394→        if not file_cache_previously_enabled:
   395→            disable_file_cache()
   396→
   397→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   398→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   399→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   400→    )
   401→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   402→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   403→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   404→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   405→    )
   406→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   407→        findings,
   408→        runtime,
   409→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   410→    )
   411→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   412→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   413→
   414→
   415→def merge_scan_results(
   416→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   417→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   418→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   419→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   420→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   421→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   422→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   423→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   424→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   425→
   426→    if not path_changed:
   427→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   428→    else:
   429→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   430→    prev_dim_scores = (
   431→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   432→    )
   433→
   434→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   435→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   436→    runtime.state["plan_sync_failed"] = False
   437→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   438→
   439→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   440→
   441→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   442→        runtime.state,
   443→        findings,
   444→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   445→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   446→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   447→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   448→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   449→            potentials=potentials,
   450→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   451→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   452→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   453→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   454→        ),
   455→    )
   456→
   457→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   458→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   459→    )
   460→    state_mod.save_state(
   461→        runtime.state,
   462→        runtime.state_path,
   463→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   464→    )
   465→
   466→    # Clear needs_rescan flag after successful scan merge (best-effort)
   467→    if runtime.config.get("needs_rescan"):
   468→        try:
   469→            runtime.config["needs_rescan"] = False
   470→            _save_config(runtime.config)
   471→        except OSError as exc:
   472→            logger.debug("Failed to clear config.needs_rescan: %s", exc)
   473→            _record_scan_warning(
   474→                runtime,
   475→                code="config_persist_failed",
   476→                message=f"could not persist scan config updates ({exc})",
   477→            )
   478→
   479→    _reconcile_plan_best_effort(runtime)
   480→
   481→    return ScanMergeResult(
   482→        diff=diff,
   483→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   484→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   485→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   486→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   487→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   488→    )
   489→
   490→
   491→def _reconcile_plan_best_effort(runtime: ScanRuntime) -> None:
   492→    """Reconcile plan state after scan with typed warning/error handling."""
   493→    from desloppify.engine.plan import (
   494→        auto_cluster_findings,
   495→        load_plan,
   496→        reconcile_plan_after_scan,
   497→        save_plan,
   498→        sync_stale_dimensions,
   499→        sync_unscored_dimensions,
   500→    )
   501→
   502→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   503→    try:
   504→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   505→        dirty = False
   506→
   507→        # Standard reconciliation (only if plan has user content)
   508→        if (
   509→            plan.get("queue_order")
   510→            or plan.get("overrides")
   511→            or plan.get("clusters")
   512→            or plan.get("skipped")
   513→        ):
   514→            recon = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, runtime.state)
   515→            if recon.changes:
   516→                dirty = True
   517→            if recon.resurfaced:
   518→                print(colorize(
   519→                    f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   520→                    "cyan",
   521→                ))
   522→
   523→        # Unscored dimension sync (prepend to front, unconditional)
   524→        unscored_sync = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state)
   525→        if unscored_sync.changes:
   526→            dirty = True
   527→        if unscored_sync.injected:
   528→            print(colorize(
   529→                f"  Plan: {len(unscored_sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
   530→                "cyan",
   531→            ))
   532→
   533→        # Stale dimension sync (always — handles both cleanup and injection)
   534→        dim_sync = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state)
   535→        if dim_sync.changes:
   536→            dirty = True
   537→        if dim_sync.pruned:
   538→            print(colorize(
   539→                f"  Plan: {len(dim_sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   540→                "cyan",
   541→            ))
   542→        if dim_sync.injected:
   543→            print(colorize(
   544→                f"  Plan: {len(dim_sync.injected)} stale subjective dimension(s) queued for refresh.",
   545→                "cyan",
   546→            ))
   547→
   548→        # Auto-cluster findings into task groups
   549→        ac_changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, runtime.state)
   550→        if ac_changes:
   551→            dirty = True
   552→
   553→        if dirty:
   554→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   555→    except _PLAN_SYNC_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   556→        runtime.state["plan_sync_failed"] = True
   557→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped due to runtime error: %s", exc)
   558→        _record_scan_warning(
   559→            runtime,
   560→            code="plan_sync_failed",
   561→            message=f"plan reconciliation skipped ({exc})",
   562→        )
   563→    except _PLAN_SYNC_UNEXPECTED_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   564→        runtime.state["plan_sync_failed"] = True
   565→        logger.warning(
   566→            "Plan reconciliation skipped due to unexpected error: %s",
   567→            exc,
   568→            exc_info=True,
   569→        )
   570→        _record_scan_warning(
   571→            runtime,
   572→            code="plan_sync_failed_unexpected",
   573→            message=f"plan reconciliation skipped due to unexpected error ({exc})",
   574→        )
   575→
   576→
   577→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   578→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   579→    config: dict[str, object],
   580→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   581→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   582→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   583→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   584→    )
   585→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   586→    open_findings = [
   587→        finding
   588→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   589→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   590→        ).values()
   591→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   592→    ]
   593→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   594→        open_findings,
   595→        budget=noise_budget,
   596→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   597→    )
   598→
   599→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   600→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   601→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   602→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   603→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   604→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   605→    )
   606→
   607→
   608→def persist_reminder_history(
   609→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   610→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   611→) -> None:
   612→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   613→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   614→        return
   615→
   616→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   617→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   618→    state_mod.save_state(
   619→        runtime.state,
   620→        runtime.state_path,
   621→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   622→    )
   623→
   624→
   625→__all__ = [
   626→    "ScanStateContractError",
   627→    "ScanMergeResult",
   628→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   629→    "ScanRuntime",
   630→    "merge_scan_results",
   631→    "persist_reminder_history",
   632→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   633→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   634→    "run_scan_generation",
   635→]
   636→

1→"""Migration/deprecation and error-strategy signals for review context."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import Protocol
     9→
    10→from desloppify.core.signal_patterns import DEPRECATION_MARKER_RE, MIGRATION_TODO_RE
    11→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    12→
    13→
    14→class MigrationLangConfig(Protocol):
    15→    """Minimal migration-related language contract expected by signal gatherers."""
    16→
    17→    migration_mixed_extensions: set[str]
    18→    migration_pattern_pairs: list[tuple[str, re.Pattern[str], re.Pattern[str]]]
    19→
    20→
    21→def gather_migration_signals_by_name(
    22→    file_contents: dict[str, str],
    23→    lang_name: str,
    24→    *,
    25→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str],
    26→) -> dict[str, object]:
    27→    """Gather migration signals using a language name."""
    28→    if not isinstance(lang_name, str) or not lang_name.strip():
    29→        raise ValueError("lang_name must be a non-empty string")
    30→    try:
    31→        lang_cfg = get_lang(lang_name)
    32→    except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    33→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported language for migration signals: {lang_name}") from exc
    34→    return gather_migration_signals_by_config(file_contents, lang_cfg, rel_fn=rel_fn)
    35→
    36→
    37→def gather_migration_signals_by_config(
    38→    file_contents: dict[str, str],
    39→    lang_cfg: MigrationLangConfig,
    40→    *,
    41→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str],
    42→) -> dict[str, object]:
    43→    """Gather migration signals using a concrete language config object."""
    44→    _validate_lang_config(lang_cfg)
    45→    return _gather_migration_signals(file_contents, lang_cfg, rel_fn=rel_fn)
    46→
    47→
    48→def gather_migration_signals(
    49→    file_contents: dict[str, str],
    50→    lang,
    51→    *,
    52→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str],
    53→) -> dict[str, object]:
    54→    """Compute migration/deprecated signals from file contents.
    55→
    56→    Returns deprecated markers, migration TODOs, pattern pairs, mixed extensions.
    57→    """
    58→    if isinstance(lang, str):
    59→        return gather_migration_signals_by_name(file_contents, lang, rel_fn=rel_fn)
    60→    return gather_migration_signals_by_config(file_contents, lang, rel_fn=rel_fn)
    61→
    62→
    63→def _validate_lang_config(lang_cfg: object) -> None:
    64→    if lang_cfg is None:
    65→        raise TypeError("lang config is required for migration signals")
    66→    if not hasattr(lang_cfg, "migration_mixed_extensions"):
    67→        raise TypeError("lang config missing 'migration_mixed_extensions'")
    68→    if not hasattr(lang_cfg, "migration_pattern_pairs"):
    69→        raise TypeError("lang config missing 'migration_pattern_pairs'")
    70→
    71→
    72→def _gather_migration_signals(
    73→    file_contents: dict[str, str],
    74→    lang_cfg: MigrationLangConfig,
    75→    *,
    76→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str],
    77→) -> dict[str, object]:
    78→    deprecated_files: dict[str, int] = {}
    79→    migration_todos: list[dict] = []
    80→    stems_by_ext: dict[str, set[str]] = {}  # stem -> set of extensions
    81→
    82→    mixed_exts = set(lang_cfg.migration_mixed_extensions or set())
    83→
    84→    for filepath, content in file_contents.items():
    85→        rpath = rel_fn(filepath)
    86→
    87→        # Deprecated markers
    88→        dep_count = len(DEPRECATION_MARKER_RE.findall(content))
    89→        if dep_count > 0:
    90→            deprecated_files[rpath] = dep_count
    91→
    92→        # Migration TODOs
    93→        for match in MIGRATION_TODO_RE.finditer(content):
    94→            migration_todos.append({"file": rpath, "text": match.group(0)[:120]})
    95→
    96→        # Track stems for mixed extension detection
    97→        path = Path(rpath)
    98→        stem = path.stem
    99→        ext = path.suffix
   100→        if ext in mixed_exts:
   101→            stems_by_ext.setdefault(stem, set()).add(ext)
   102→
   103→    # Pattern pair detection
   104→    pairs = list(lang_cfg.migration_pattern_pairs or [])
   105→
   106→    pattern_results: list[dict] = []
   107→    for name, old_re, new_re in pairs:
   108→        old_count = sum(
   109→            1 for content in file_contents.values() if old_re.search(content)
   110→        )
   111→        new_count = sum(
   112→            1 for content in file_contents.values() if new_re.search(content)
   113→        )
   114→        if old_count > 0 and new_count > 0:
   115→            pattern_results.append(
   116→                {
   117→                    "name": name,
   118→                    "old_count": old_count,
   119→                    "new_count": new_count,
   120→                }
   121→            )
   122→
   123→    # Mixed extensions
   124→    mixed_stems = sorted(stem for stem, exts in stems_by_ext.items() if len(exts) >= 2)
   125→
   126→    result: dict[str, object] = {}
   127→    if deprecated_files:
   128→        result["deprecated_markers"] = {
   129→            "total": sum(deprecated_files.values()),
   130→            "files": deprecated_files,
   131→        }
   132→    if migration_todos:
   133→        result["migration_todos"] = migration_todos[:30]
   134→    if pattern_results:
   135→        result["pattern_pairs"] = pattern_results
   136→    if mixed_stems:
   137→        result["mixed_extensions"] = mixed_stems[:20]
   138→    return result
   139→
   140→
   141→def classify_error_strategy(content: str) -> str | None:
   142→    """Classify a file's primary error handling strategy."""
   143→    throws = len(re.findall(r"\b(?:throw\s+new|raise\s+\w)", content))
   144→    returns_null = len(re.findall(r"\breturn\s+(?:null|None|undefined)\b", content))
   145→    result_type = len(re.findall(r"\b(?:Result|Either|Ok|Err)\b", content))
   146→    try_catch = len(re.findall(r"\b(?:try\s*\{|try\s*:)", content))
   147→
   148→    counts = {
   149→        "throw": throws,
   150→        "return_null": returns_null,
   151→        "result_type": result_type,
   152→        "try_catch": try_catch,
   153→    }
   154→    total = sum(counts.values())
   155→    if total == 0:
   156→        return None
   157→    dominant = max(counts.items(), key=lambda item: item[1])[0]
   158→    # "mixed" if no strategy accounts for >60% of occurrences
   159→    if counts[dominant] / total < 0.6:
   160→        return "mixed"
   161→    return dominant
   162→
   163→
   164→__all__ = [
   165→    "MigrationLangConfig",
   166→    "classify_error_strategy",
   167→    "gather_migration_signals",
   168→    "gather_migration_signals_by_name",
   169→    "gather_migration_signals_by_config",
   170→]
   171→

    61→@dataclass
    62→class ReviewPrepareOptions:

    76→

    85→def _rel_list(s) -> list[str]:
    86→    """Normalize a set or list of paths to sorted relative paths (max 10)."""
    87→    if isinstance(s, set):
    88→        return sorted(rel(x) for x in s)[:10]
    89→    return [rel(x) for x in list(s)[:10]]
    90→
    91→
    92→def _normalize_max_files(value: Any) -> int | None:
    93→    """Normalize max_files input: None/<=0 means unlimited."""
    94→    if value in (None, ""):
    95→        return None
    96→    parsed = int(value)
    97→    return parsed if parsed > 0 else None
    98→
    99→
   100→def _relative_to_base(filepath: str, *, resolved_base: Path) -> str | None:
   101→    """Return ``filepath`` relative to ``resolved_base`` when inside the base tree."""
   102→    try:
   103→        resolved = Path(filepath).resolve()
   104→        return resolved.relative_to(resolved_base).as_posix()
   105→    except ValueError:
   106→        return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def _collect_allowed_review_files(
   110→    files: list[str],
   111→    lang: object,
   112→    *,
   113→    base_path: Path | None = None,
   114→) -> set[str]:
   115→    """Return relative production-file paths allowed for holistic review batches."""
   116→    allowed: set[str] = set()
   117→    zone_map = getattr(lang, "zone_map", None)
   118→    resolved_base = base_path.resolve() if isinstance(base_path, Path) else None
   119→    for filepath in files:
   120→        if not isinstance(filepath, str):
   121→            continue
   122→        normalized = filepath.strip().replace("\\", "/")
   123→        if not normalized:
   124→            continue
   125→        if zone_map is not None:
   126→            try:
   127→                zone = zone_map.get(filepath)
   128→                zone_value = getattr(zone, "value", str(zone))
   129→            except (AttributeError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
   130→                zone_value = "production"
   131→            if zone_value in _NON_PRODUCTION_ZONES:
   132→                continue
   133→        allowed.add(normalized)
   134→        allowed.add(rel(filepath))
   135→        if resolved_base is not None:
   136→            relative = _relative_to_base(filepath, resolved_base=resolved_base)
   137→            if relative is not None:
   138→                allowed.add(relative)
   139→    return allowed
   140→
   141→
   142→def _file_in_allowed_scope(filepath: object, allowed_files: set[str]) -> bool:
   143→    """True when *filepath* resolves to a currently in-scope review file."""
   144→    if not isinstance(filepath, str):
   145→        return False
   146→    normalized = filepath.strip().replace("\\", "/")
   147→    if not normalized:
   148→        return False
   149→    if normalized in allowed_files:
   150→        return True
