1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    empty_plan,
    10→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16→# Helpers
    17→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→
    19→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    20→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    21→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    22→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    if finding_id in order:
    24→        order.remove(finding_id)
    25→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_position(
    29→    order: list[str],
    30→    position: str,
    31→    target: str | None = None,
    32→    offset: int | None = None,
    33→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    34→) -> int:
    35→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    36→
    37→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    38→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    39→    """
    40→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    41→
    42→    if position == "top":
    43→        return 0
    44→    if position == "bottom":
    45→        return len(order)
    46→
    47→    if position == "before" and target:
    48→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    49→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    50→                return i
    51→        return 0
    52→
    53→    if position == "after" and target:
    54→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    55→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    56→                return i + 1
    57→        return len(order)
    58→
    59→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    60→        if not finding_ids:
    61→            return 0
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    64→        current_idx_clean = None
    65→        non_moving_seen = 0
    66→        for item_id in order:
    67→            if item_id == first_id:
    68→                current_idx_clean = non_moving_seen
    69→                break
    70→            if item_id not in moving:
    71→                non_moving_seen += 1
    72→        if current_idx_clean is None:
    73→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    74→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    75→        return max(0, current_idx_clean - offset)
    76→
    77→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    78→        if not finding_ids:
    79→            return len(order)
    80→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    81→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    82→        current_idx_clean = None
    83→        non_moving_seen = 0
    84→        for item_id in order:
    85→            if item_id == first_id:
    86→                current_idx_clean = non_moving_seen
    87→                break
    88→            if item_id not in moving:
    89→                non_moving_seen += 1
    90→        if current_idx_clean is None:
    91→            return len(clean_order)
    92→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx_clean + offset)
    93→
    94→    return len(order)
    95→
    96→
    97→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98→# Move items
    99→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100→
   101→def move_items(
   102→    plan: PlanModel,
   103→    finding_ids: list[str],
   104→    position: str,
   105→    target: str | None = None,
   106→    offset: int | None = None,
   107→) -> int:
   108→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   109→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   110→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   111→
   112→    # Remove from skipped if present
   113→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   114→    for fid in finding_ids:
   115→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   116→
   117→    # Remove from current position in order
   118→    for fid in finding_ids:
   119→        if fid in order:
   120→            order.remove(fid)
   121→
   122→    # Resolve insertion point
   123→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   124→
   125→    # Insert in original order
   126→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   127→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   128→
   129→    return len(finding_ids)
   130→
   131→
   132→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   133→# Skip / unskip
   134→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   135→
   136→def skip_items(
   137→    plan: PlanModel,
   138→    finding_ids: list[str],
   139→    *,
   140→    kind: str = "temporary",
   141→    reason: str | None = None,
   142→    note: str | None = None,
   143→    attestation: str | None = None,
   144→    review_after: int | None = None,
   145→    scan_count: int = 0,
   146→) -> int:
   147→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   148→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   149→    now = utc_now()
   150→    count = 0
   151→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   152→    for fid in finding_ids:
   153→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   154→        skipped[fid] = {
   155→            "finding_id": fid,
   156→            "kind": kind,
   157→            "reason": reason,
   158→            "note": note,
   159→            "attestation": attestation,
   160→            "created_at": now,
   161→            "review_after": review_after,
   162→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   163→        }
   164→        count += 1
   165→    return count
   166→
   167→
   168→def unskip_items(
   169→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   170→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   171→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   172→
   173→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   174→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   175→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   176→    """
   177→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   178→    count = 0
   179→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   180→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   181→    for fid in finding_ids:
   182→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   183→        if entry is not None:
   184→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   185→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   186→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   187→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   188→            count += 1
   189→    return count, need_reopen
   190→
   191→
   192→def resurface_stale_skips(
   193→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   194→) -> list[str]:
   195→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   196→
   197→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   198→    """
   199→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   200→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   201→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   202→    for fid in list(skipped):
   203→        entry = skipped[fid]
   204→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   205→            continue
   206→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   207→        if review_after is None:
   208→            continue
   209→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   210→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   211→            skipped.pop(fid)
   212→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   213→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   214→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   215→    return resurfaced
   216→
   217→
   218→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   219→# Describe / annotate
   220→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   221→
   222→def describe_finding(
   223→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   224→) -> None:
   225→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   226→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   227→    now = utc_now()
   228→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   229→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   230→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   231→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   232→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   233→
   234→
   235→def annotate_finding(
   236→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   237→) -> None:
   238→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   239→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   240→    now = utc_now()
   241→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   242→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   243→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   244→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   245→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   246→
   247→
   248→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   249→# Clusters
   250→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   251→
   252→def create_cluster(
   253→    plan: PlanModel,
   254→    name: str,
   255→    description: str | None = None,
   256→    action: str | None = None,
   257→) -> Cluster:
   258→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   259→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   260→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   261→        raise ValueError(
   262→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   263→        )
   264→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   265→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   266→    now = utc_now()
   267→    cluster: Cluster = {
   268→        "name": name,
   269→        "description": description,
   270→        "finding_ids": [],
   271→        "created_at": now,
   272→        "updated_at": now,
   273→        "auto": False,
   274→        "cluster_key": "",
   275→        "action": action,
   276→        "user_modified": False,
   277→    }
   278→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   279→    return cluster
   280→
   281→
   282→def add_to_cluster(
   283→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   284→) -> int:
   285→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   286→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   287→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   288→    if cluster is None:
   289→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   290→
   291→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   292→    count = 0
   293→    now = utc_now()
   294→    for fid in finding_ids:
   295→        if fid not in member_ids:
   296→            member_ids.append(fid)
   297→            count += 1
   298→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   299→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   300→        if fid not in overrides:
   301→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   302→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   303→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   304→
   305→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   306→    return count
   307→
   308→
   309→def remove_from_cluster(
   310→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   311→) -> int:
   312→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   313→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   314→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   315→    if cluster is None:
   316→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   317→
   318→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   319→    now = utc_now()
   320→    count = 0
   321→    for fid in finding_ids:
   322→        if fid in member_ids:
   323→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   324→            count += 1
   325→        # Clear cluster from override
   326→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   327→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   328→            override["cluster"] = None
   329→            override["updated_at"] = now
   330→
   331→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   332→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   333→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   334→
   335→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   336→    return count
   337→
   338→
   339→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   340→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   341→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   342→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   343→    if cluster is None:
   344→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   345→
   346→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   347→    now = utc_now()
   348→    for fid in orphaned:
   349→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   350→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   351→            override["cluster"] = None
   352→            override["updated_at"] = now
   353→
   354→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   355→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   356→
   357→    return orphaned
   358→
   359→
   360→def move_cluster(
   361→    plan: PlanModel,
   362→    cluster_name: str,
   363→    position: str,
   364→    target: str | None = None,
   365→    offset: int | None = None,
   366→) -> int:
   367→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   368→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   369→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   370→    if cluster is None:
   371→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   372→
   373→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   374→    if not member_ids:
   375→        return 0
   376→
   377→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   378→
   379→
   380→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   381→# Focus
   382→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   383→
   384→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   385→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   386→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   387→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   388→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   389→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   390→
   391→
   392→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   393→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   394→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   395→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   396→
   397→
   398→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   399→# Reset
   400→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   401→
   402→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   403→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   404→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   405→    plan.clear()
   406→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   407→        plan[k] = v
   408→    plan["created"] = created
   409→
   410→
   411→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   412→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   413→
   414→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   415→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   416→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   417→    """
   418→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   419→    found = 0
   420→
   421→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   422→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   423→    for fid in finding_ids:
   424→        was_present = False
   425→        if fid in order:
   426→            order.remove(fid)
   427→            was_present = True
   428→        if fid in skipped:
   429→            skipped.pop(fid)
   430→            was_present = True
   431→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   432→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   433→            if fid in ids:
   434→                ids.remove(fid)
   435→                was_present = True
   436→        if was_present:
   437→            found += 1
   438→
   439→    return found
   440→
   441→
   442→__all__ = [
   443→    "add_to_cluster",
   444→    "annotate_finding",
   445→    "clear_focus",
   446→    "create_cluster",
   447→    "delete_cluster",
   448→    "describe_finding",
   449→    "move_cluster",
   450→    "move_items",
   451→    "purge_ids",
   452→    "remove_from_cluster",
   453→    "reset_plan",
   454→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   455→    "set_focus",
   456→    "skip_items",
   457→    "unskip_items",
   458→]
   459→
```

    31→# ── Shared helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    32→
    33→
    34→def abs_path(filepath: str) -> str:
    35→    """Resolve filepath to absolute using resolve_path."""
    36→    return resolve_path(filepath)
    37→
    38→
    39→def file_excerpt(filepath: str, max_lines: int = 30) -> str | None:
    40→    """Read first *max_lines* of a file, returning the text or None."""
    41→    content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
    42→    if content is None:
    43→        return None
    44→    lines = content.splitlines(keepends=True)
    45→    if len(lines) <= max_lines:
    46→        return content
    47→    return "".join(lines[:max_lines]) + f"\n... ({len(lines) - max_lines} more lines)"
    48→
    49→
    50→def dep_graph_lookup(
    51→    graph: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
    52→    filepath: str,
    53→) -> dict[str, object]:
    54→    """Look up a file in the dep graph, trying absolute and relative keys."""
    55→    resolved = resolve_path(filepath)
    56→    entry = graph.get(resolved)
    57→    if entry is not None:
    58→        return entry
    59→    # Try relative path
    60→    rpath = rel(filepath)
    61→    entry = graph.get(rpath)
    62→    if entry is not None:
    63→        return entry
    64→    return {}
    65→
    66→
    67→def importer_count(entry: dict) -> int:
    68→    """Extract importer count from a dep graph entry."""
    69→    importers = entry.get("importers", set())
    70→    if isinstance(importers, set):
    71→        return len(importers)
    72→    return entry.get("importer_count", 0)
    73→
    74→
    75→# ── Per-file review context builder ──────────────────────────────
    76→
    77→
    78→def build_review_context(
    79→    path: Path,
    80→    lang,
    81→    state: dict,
    82→    files: list[str] | None = None,
    83→) -> ReviewContext:
    84→    """Gather codebase conventions for contextual evaluation.
    85→
    86→    If *files* is provided, skip file_finder (avoids redundant filesystem walks).
    87→    """
    88→    if files is None:
    89→        files = lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else []
    90→    ctx = ReviewContext()
    91→
    92→    if not files:
    93→        return ctx
    94→
    95→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
    96→    if not already_cached:
    97→        enable_file_cache()
    98→    try:
    99→        return _build_review_context_inner(files, lang, state, ctx)
   100→    finally:

1→"""Holistic investigation batch builders for review preparation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review._context.models import HolisticContext
     8→
     9→_EXTENSIONLESS_FILENAMES = {
    10→    "makefile",
    11→    "dockerfile",
    12→    "readme",
    13→    "license",
    14→    "build",
    15→    "workspace",
    16→}
    17→
    18→_GOVERNANCE_REFERENCE_FILES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    19→    "README.md",
    20→    "DEVELOPMENT_PHILOSOPHY.md",
    21→    "desloppify/README.md",
    22→    "pyproject.toml",
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def _normalize_file_path(value: object) -> str | None:
    27→    """Normalize/validate candidate file paths for batch payloads."""
    28→    if not isinstance(value, str):
    29→        return None
    30→    text = value.strip().strip(",'\"")
    31→    if not text or text in {".", ".."}:
    32→        return None
    33→    if text.endswith("/"):
    34→        return None
    35→
    36→    basename = Path(text).name
    37→    if not basename:
    38→        return None
    39→    if "." not in basename and basename.lower() not in _EXTENSIONLESS_FILENAMES:
    40→        return None
    41→    return text
    42→
    43→
    44→def _collect_unique_files(
    45→    sources: list[list[dict]],
    46→    key: str = "file",
    47→    *,
    48→    max_files: int | None = None,
    49→) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Collect unique file paths from multiple source lists."""
    51→    seen: set[str] = set()
    52→    out: list[str] = []
    53→    for src in sources:
    54→        for item in src:
    55→            f = _normalize_file_path(item.get(key, ""))
    56→            if f and f not in seen:
    57→                seen.add(f)
    58→                out.append(f)
    59→                if max_files is not None and len(out) >= max_files:
    60→                    return out
    61→    return out
    62→
    63→
    64→def _existing_repo_files(
    65→    repo_root: Path | None,
    66→    candidates: tuple[str, ...],
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Return repository-relative paths for candidate files that exist."""
    69→    if repo_root is None:
    70→        return []
    71→    out: list[str] = []
    72→    for candidate in candidates:
    73→        if (repo_root / candidate).is_file():
    74→            out.append(candidate)
    75→    return out
    76→
    77→
    78→def _collect_files_from_batches(
    79→    batches: list[dict], *, max_files: int | None = None
    80→) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Collect unique file paths across batch payloads (preserving order)."""
    82→    seen: set[str] = set()
    83→    out: list[str] = []
    84→    for batch in batches:
    85→        for filepath in batch.get("files_to_read", []):
    86→            normalized = _normalize_file_path(filepath)
    87→            if not normalized:
    88→                continue
    89→            if normalized in seen:
    90→                continue
    91→            seen.add(normalized)
    92→            out.append(normalized)
    93→            if max_files is not None and len(out) >= max_files:
    94→                return out
    95→    return out
    96→
    97→
    98→def _representative_files_for_directory(
    99→    ctx: HolisticContext,
   100→    directory: str,
   101→    *,
   102→    max_files: int = 3,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Map a directory-level signal to representative file paths."""
   105→    if not isinstance(directory, str) or not directory.strip():
   106→        return []
   107→
   108→    dir_key = directory.strip()
   109→    if dir_key in {".", "./"}:
   110→        normalized_dir = "."
   111→    else:
   112→        normalized_dir = f"{dir_key.rstrip('/')}/"
   113→
   114→    profiles = ctx.structure.get("directory_profiles", {})
   115→    profile = profiles.get(normalized_dir)
   116→    if not isinstance(profile, dict):
   117→        return []
   118→
   119→    out: list[str] = []
   120→    for filename in profile.get("files", []):
   121→        if not isinstance(filename, str) or not filename:
   122→            continue
   123→        filepath = (
   124→            filename
   125→            if normalized_dir == "."
   126→            else f"{normalized_dir.rstrip('/')}/{filename}"
   127→        )
   128→        normalized = _normalize_file_path(filepath)
   129→        if not normalized or normalized in out:
   130→            continue
   131→        out.append(normalized)
   132→        if len(out) >= max_files:
   133→            break
   134→    return out
   135→
   136→
   137→def _batch_arch_coupling(ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None) -> dict:
   138→    """Batch 1: Architecture & Coupling - god modules, import-time side effects."""
   139→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   140→        [
   141→            ctx.architecture.get("god_modules", []),
   142→            ctx.coupling.get("module_level_io", []),
   143→            ctx.coupling.get("boundary_violations", []),
   144→            ctx.dependencies.get("deferred_import_density", []),
   145→        ],
   146→        max_files=max_files,
   147→    )
   148→    return {
   149→        "name": "Architecture & Coupling",
   150→        "dimensions": ["cross_module_architecture", "high_level_elegance"],
   151→        "files_to_read": files,
   152→        "why": "god modules, import-time side effects, boundary violations, deferred import pressure",
   153→    }
   154→
   155→
   156→def _batch_conventions_errors(
   157→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   158→) -> dict:
   159→    """Batch 2: Conventions & Errors - sibling behavior outliers, mixed strategies."""
   160→    sibling = ctx.conventions.get("sibling_behavior", {})
   161→    outlier_files = [
   162→        {"file": o["file"]} for di in sibling.values() for o in di.get("outliers", [])
   163→    ]
   164→    error_dirs = ctx.errors.get("strategy_by_directory", {})
   165→    mixed_dir_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   166→    for directory, strategies in error_dirs.items():
   167→        if not isinstance(strategies, dict) or len(strategies) < 3:
   168→            continue
   169→        for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   170→            mixed_dir_files.append({"file": filepath})
   171→
   172→    exception_files = [
   173→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   174→        for item in ctx.errors.get("exception_hotspots", [])
   175→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   176→    ]
   177→    dupe_files = [
   178→        {"file": item.get("files", [""])[0]}
   179→        for item in ctx.conventions.get("duplicate_clusters", [])
   180→        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("files")
   181→    ]
   182→    naming_drift_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   183→    for entry in ctx.conventions.get("naming_drift", []):
   184→        if isinstance(entry, dict):
   185→            directory = entry.get("directory", "")
   186→            for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   187→                naming_drift_files.append({"file": filepath})
   188→
   189→    files = _collect_unique_files(
   190→        [outlier_files, mixed_dir_files, exception_files, dupe_files, naming_drift_files],
   191→        max_files=max_files,
   192→    )
   193→    return {
   194→        "name": "Conventions & Errors",
   195→        "dimensions": ["convention_outlier", "error_consistency", "mid_level_elegance"],
   196→        "files_to_read": files,
   197→        "why": "naming drift, behavioral outliers, mixed error strategies, exception hotspots, duplicate clusters",
   198→    }
   199→
   200→
   201→def _batch_abstractions_deps(
   202→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   203→) -> dict:
   204→    """Batch 3: Abstractions & Dependencies - abstraction hotspots, dep cycles."""
   205→    util_files = ctx.abstractions.get("util_files", [])
   206→    wrapper_files = [
   207→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   208→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("pass_through_wrappers", [])
   209→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   210→    ]
   211→    indirection_files = [
   212→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   213→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("indirection_hotspots", [])
   214→        if isinstance(item, dict)
   215→    ]
   216→    param_bag_files = [
   217→        {"file": item.get("file", "")}
   218→        for item in ctx.abstractions.get("wide_param_bags", [])
   219→        if isinstance(item, dict)

   221→    interface_files: list[dict[str, str]] = []
   222→    for item in ctx.abstractions.get("one_impl_interfaces", []):
   223→        if not isinstance(item, dict):

   225→        for group in ("declared_in", "implemented_in"):
   226→            for filepath in item.get(group, []):
   227→                interface_files.append({"file": filepath})
   228→
   229→    delegation_files = [

   251→    cycle_files = [

   328→    migration = ctx.migration_signals
   329→    debt_files: list[dict] = []

   351→def _batch_package_organization(
   352→    ctx: HolisticContext, *, max_files: int | None = None
   353→) -> dict:
   354→    """Batch 7: Package Organization - file placement, directory boundaries."""
   355→    structure = ctx.structure
   356→    struct_files: list[dict] = []
   357→    # Add flat_dir_findings directory representatives
   358→    for entry in structure.get("flat_dir_findings", []):
   359→        if isinstance(entry, dict):
   360→            directory = entry.get("directory", "")
   361→            for filepath in _representative_files_for_directory(ctx, directory):
   362→                struct_files.append({"file": filepath})
   363→    for rf in structure.get("root_files", []):
   364→        if rf.get("role") == "peripheral":
   365→            struct_files.append({"file": rf["file"]})
   366→    dir_profiles = structure.get("directory_profiles", {})
   367→    largest_dirs = sorted(
   368→        dir_profiles.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1].get("file_count", 0)
   369→    )[:3]
   370→    for dir_key, profile in largest_dirs:
   371→        for fname in profile.get("files", [])[:3]:
   372→            dir_path = dir_key.rstrip("/")
   373→            rpath = f"{dir_path}/{fname}" if dir_path != "." else fname
   374→            struct_files.append({"file": rpath})
   375→    coupling_matrix = structure.get("coupling_matrix", {})
   376→    seen_edges: set[str] = set()
   377→    for edge in coupling_matrix:
   378→        if " → " in edge:
   379→            a, b = edge.split(" → ", 1)
   380→            reverse = f"{b} → {a}"
   381→            if reverse in coupling_matrix and edge not in seen_edges:
   382→                seen_edges.add(edge)
   383→                seen_edges.add(reverse)
   384→                for d in (a, b):
   385→                    for fname in dir_profiles.get(d, {}).get("files", [])[:2]:
   386→                        dir_path = d.rstrip("/")
   387→                        rpath = f"{dir_path}/{fname}" if dir_path != "." else fname
   388→                        struct_files.append({"file": rpath})
   389→    files = _collect_unique_files([struct_files], max_files=max_files)
   390→    return {
   391→        "name": "Package Organization",
   392→        "dimensions": ["package_organization", "high_level_elegance"],
   393→        "files_to_read": files,
   394→        "why": "file placement, directory boundaries, architectural layering",
   395→    }
   396→

   400→    evidence = ctx.scan_evidence

   422→    return {

   430→def _batch_governance_contracts(
   431→    ctx: HolisticContext,
   432→    *,
   433→    repo_root: Path | None,
   434→    max_files: int | None = None,
   435→) -> dict:
   436→    """Batch 8: Governance & Contracts - docs/policy promises vs runtime posture."""
   437→    docs = _existing_repo_files(repo_root, _GOVERNANCE_REFERENCE_FILES)
   438→    if not docs:
   439→        return {
   440→            "name": "Governance & Contracts",

   442→                "cross_module_architecture",
   443→                "high_level_elegance",
   444→                "test_strategy",
   445→                "package_organization",
   446→            ],
   447→            "files_to_read": [],
   448→            "why": "architecture contracts, compatibility policy, docs-vs-runtime scope, and quality-gate coverage",
   449→        }
   450→    top_imported = [
   451→        {"file": filepath}
   452→        for filepath in list(ctx.architecture.get("top_imported", {}).keys())[:5]
   453→        if isinstance(filepath, str)
   454→    ]
   455→    anchor_files = _collect_unique_files(
   456→        [
   457→            top_imported,
   458→            ctx.architecture.get("god_modules", []),
   459→            ctx.coupling.get("module_level_io", []),
   460→        ],
   461→        max_files=5,
   462→    )
   463→    seen = set(docs)
   464→    files = list(docs)
   465→    for filepath in anchor_files:
   466→        if filepath in seen:
   467→            continue
   468→        seen.add(filepath)
   469→        files.append(filepath)
   470→    if max_files is not None:
   471→        files = files[:max_files]
   472→    return {
   473→        "name": "Governance & Contracts",
   474→        "dimensions": [
   475→            "cross_module_architecture",
   476→            "high_level_elegance",
   477→            "test_strategy",
   478→            "package_organization",
   479→        ],
   480→        "files_to_read": files,
   481→        "why": "architecture contracts, compatibility policy, docs-vs-runtime scope, and quality-gate coverage",
   482→    }

   512→            repo_root=repo_root,
   513→            max_files=max_files_per_batch,

   515→    ]
   516→    return [batch for batch in batches if batch["files_to_read"]]

1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_unscored_ids,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    17→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    18→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    19→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    20→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    21→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    22→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1

    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Grouping key computation
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    30→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    31→
    32→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    33→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    34→    """
    35→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    36→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    37→    if kind:
    38→        return kind
    39→
    40→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    41→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    42→    parts = fid.split("::")
    43→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    44→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    45→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    46→        candidate = parts[-1]
    47→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    48→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    49→            return candidate
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    54→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    55→
    56→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    57→    """
    58→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→
    60→    if meta is None:
    61→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    62→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    63→
    64→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    65→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    66→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    67→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    68→        if dimension:
    69→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    70→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    71→
    72→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    73→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    74→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    75→    ):
    76→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    77→        if file:
    78→            normalized = str(file).strip().replace("\\", "/")
    79→            if normalized:
    80→                return f"file::{detector}::{normalized}"
    81→
    82→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    83→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    84→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    85→        if subtype:
    86→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    87→
    88→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    89→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    90→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    91→
    92→    # Everything else → by detector
    93→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    94→
    95→
    96→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    97→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    98→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    99→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
   100→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   101→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   102→    # detector::security → auto/security
   103→    parts = key.split("::")
   104→    if len(parts) == 2:
   105→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   106→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   107→        if prefix_type == "review":
   108→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   109→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   110→    if len(parts) == 3:
   111→        if parts[0] == "file":
   112→            safe_path = parts[2].replace("/", "__").replace("\\", "__")
   113→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{safe_path}"
   114→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   115→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   116→
   117→
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→# Description and action generation
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→def _generate_description(
   123→    cluster_name: str,
   124→    members: list[dict],
   125→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   126→    subtype: str | None,
   127→) -> str:
   128→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   129→    count = len(members)
   130→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   131→
   132→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   133→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   134→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   135→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   136→
   137→    if detector == "structural":
   138→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   139→        if len(files) == 1:
   140→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   141→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   142→
   143→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   144→    if subtype:
   145→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   146→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   147→
   148→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   149→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   150→
   151→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   152→
   153→
   154→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   155→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   156→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   157→        return None
   158→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   159→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   160→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   161→        return fixer_name
   162→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   163→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   164→        if subtype in fixer:
   165→            return fixer
   166→    return None
   167→
   168→
   169→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   170→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   171→
   172→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   173→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   174→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   175→    """
   176→    if " — " in guidance:
   177→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   178→    return guidance
   179→
   180→
   181→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   182→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   183→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   184→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   185→}
   186→
   187→
   188→def _generate_action(
   189→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   190→    subtype: str | None,
   191→) -> str:
   192→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   193→
   194→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   195→    """
   196→    if meta is None:
   197→        return "review and fix each finding"
   198→
   199→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   200→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   201→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   202→        if matched_fixer:
   203→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   204→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   205→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   206→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   207→
   208→    if meta.tool == "move":
   209→        return "desloppify move"
   210→
   211→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   212→    if meta.guidance:
   213→        if subtype:
   214→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   215→        return meta.guidance
   216→
   217→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   218→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   219→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   220→    )
   221→
   222→
   223→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   224→# Main algorithm
   225→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   226→
   227→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   228→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   229→
   230→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   231→    """
   232→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   233→    changes = 0
   234→
   235→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   236→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   237→
   238→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   239→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   240→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   241→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   242→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   243→
   244→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   245→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   246→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   247→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   248→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   249→            continue
   250→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   251→            continue
   252→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   253→            continue
   254→
   255→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   256→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   257→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   258→        if key is None:
   259→            continue
   260→
   261→        groups[key].append(fid)
   262→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   263→
   264→    # Drop singleton groups
   265→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   266→
   267→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   268→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   269→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   270→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   271→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   272→            if ck:
   273→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   274→
   275→    now = utc_now()
   276→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   277→
   278→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   279→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   280→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   281→
   282→        # Representative finding for metadata
   283→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   284→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   285→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   286→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   287→
   288→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   289→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   290→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   291→
   292→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   293→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   294→
   295→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   296→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   297→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   298→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   299→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   300→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   301→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   302→                if new_ids:
   303→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   304→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   305→                    changes += 1
   306→            else:
   307→                # Replace membership wholesale
   308→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   309→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   310→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   311→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   312→                    cluster["description"] = description
   313→                    cluster["action"] = action
   314→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   315→                    changes += 1
   316→        else:
   317→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   318→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   319→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   320→                # Append a disambiguator
   321→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   322→
   323→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   324→                "name": cluster_name,
   325→                "description": description,
   326→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   327→                "created_at": now,
   328→                "updated_at": now,
   329→                "auto": True,
   330→                "cluster_key": key,
   331→                "action": action,
   332→                "user_modified": False,
   333→            }
   334→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   335→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   336→            changes += 1
   337→
   338→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   339→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   340→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   341→        for fid in member_ids:
   342→            if fid not in overrides:
   343→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   344→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   345→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   346→
   347→    # --- Subjective dimension clusters (unscored + stale) -------------------
   348→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   349→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   350→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   351→    )
   352→    unscored_state_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   353→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   354→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   355→    )
   356→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   357→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids
   358→    )
   359→
   360→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   361→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   362→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   363→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   364→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   365→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   366→
   367→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   368→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   369→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   370→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   371→            new_ids = set(unscored_queue_ids)
   372→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   373→                cluster["finding_ids"] = unscored_queue_ids
   374→                cluster["description"] = description
   375→                cluster["action"] = action
   376→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   377→                changes += 1
   378→        else:
   379→            clusters[_UNSCORED_NAME] = {
   380→                "name": _UNSCORED_NAME,
   381→                "description": description,
   382→                "finding_ids": unscored_queue_ids,
   383→                "created_at": now,
   384→                "updated_at": now,
   385→                "auto": True,
   386→                "cluster_key": _UNSCORED_KEY,
   387→                "action": action,
   388→                "user_modified": False,
   389→            }
   390→            existing_by_key[_UNSCORED_KEY] = _UNSCORED_NAME
   391→            changes += 1
   392→
   393→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   394→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY, _UNSCORED_NAME)
   395→        for fid in unscored_queue_ids:
   396→            if fid not in overrides:
   397→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   398→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   399→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   400→
   401→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   402→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   403→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   404→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   405→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   406→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   407→
   408→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   409→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   410→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   411→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   412→            new_ids = set(stale_queue_ids)
   413→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   414→                cluster["finding_ids"] = stale_queue_ids
   415→                cluster["description"] = description
   416→                cluster["action"] = action
   417→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   418→                changes += 1
   419→        else:
   420→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   421→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   422→                "description": description,
   423→                "finding_ids": stale_queue_ids,
   424→                "created_at": now,
   425→                "updated_at": now,
   426→                "auto": True,
   427→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   428→                "action": action,
   429→                "user_modified": False,
   430→            }
   431→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   432→            changes += 1
   433→
   434→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   435→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   436→        for fid in stale_queue_ids:
   437→            if fid not in overrides:
   438→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   439→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   440→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   441→
   442→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   443→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   444→        cluster = clusters[name]
   445→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   446→            continue
   447→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   448→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   449→            continue
   450→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   451→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   452→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   453→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   454→            if alive:
   455→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   456→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   457→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   458→                    changes += 1
   459→                continue
   460→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   461→        # Delete stale cluster
   462→        del clusters[name]
   463→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   464→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   465→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   466→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   467→                override["cluster"] = None
   468→                override["updated_at"] = now
   469→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   470→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   471→        changes += 1
   472→
   473→    plan["updated"] = now
   474→    return changes
   475→
   476→
   477→__all__ = [
   478→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   479→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   480→]
   481→

1→"""Directory-level structure/coupling context for holistic review."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import Counter
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel, resolve_path
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.review.context import importer_count
    11→
    12→
    13→def compute_structure_context(
    14→    file_contents: dict[str, str], lang: Any
    15→) -> dict[str, object]:
    16→    """Compute directory profiles, root-level file analysis, and coupling matrix."""
    17→    graph = lang.dep_graph or {}
    18→    structure: dict[str, object] = {}
    19→
    20→    file_info: dict[str, dict] = {}
    21→    for filepath, content in file_contents.items():
    22→        rel_path = rel(filepath)
    23→        loc = len(content.splitlines())
    24→        entry = graph.get(resolve_path(filepath), {})
    25→        fan_in = importer_count(entry)
    26→        imports_raw = entry.get("imports", set())
    27→        fan_out = (
    28→            len(imports_raw)
    29→            if isinstance(imports_raw, set)
    30→            else entry.get("import_count", 0)
    31→        )
    32→        file_info[rel_path] = {"loc": loc, "fan_in": fan_in, "fan_out": fan_out}
    33→
    34→    dir_files: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    35→    for rel_path in file_info:
    36→        parts = Path(rel_path).parts
    37→        dir_key = "." if len(parts) == 1 else str(Path(*parts[:-1])) + "/"
    38→        dir_files.setdefault(dir_key, []).append(rel_path)
    39→
    40→    dir_profiles: dict[str, dict] = {}
    41→    for dir_key, files_in_dir in dir_files.items():
    42→        if len(files_in_dir) < 2:
    43→            continue
    44→
    45→        total_loc = sum(file_info[file]["loc"] for file in files_in_dir)
    46→        avg_fan_in = sum(file_info[file]["fan_in"] for file in files_in_dir) / len(
    47→            files_in_dir
    48→        )
    49→        avg_fan_out = sum(file_info[file]["fan_out"] for file in files_in_dir) / len(
    50→            files_in_dir
    51→        )
    52→
    53→        imports_from: Counter = Counter()
    54→        imported_by: Counter = Counter()
    55→        for file in files_in_dir:
    56→            entry = graph.get(resolve_path(file), {})
    57→            for imp in entry.get("imports", set()):
    58→                imp_rel = rel(imp)
    59→                imp_parts = Path(imp_rel).parts
    60→                imp_dir = (
    61→                    str(Path(*imp_parts[:-1])) + "/" if len(imp_parts) > 1 else "."
    62→                )
    63→                if imp_dir != dir_key:
    64→                    imports_from[imp_dir] += 1
    65→            for imp in entry.get("importers", set()):
    66→                imp_rel = rel(imp)
    67→                imp_parts = Path(imp_rel).parts
    68→                imp_dir = (
    69→                    str(Path(*imp_parts[:-1])) + "/" if len(imp_parts) > 1 else "."
    70→                )
    71→                if imp_dir != dir_key:
    72→                    imported_by[imp_dir] += 1
    73→
    74→        zone_counts: Counter = Counter()
    75→        if lang.zone_map is not None:
    76→            for file in files_in_dir:
    77→                zone_counts[lang.zone_map.get(file).value] += 1
    78→
    79→        dir_profiles[dir_key] = {
    80→            "file_count": len(files_in_dir),
    81→            "files": [Path(file).name for file in sorted(files_in_dir)],
    82→            "total_loc": total_loc,
    83→            "avg_fan_in": round(avg_fan_in, 1),
    84→            "avg_fan_out": round(avg_fan_out, 1),
    85→        }
    86→        if zone_counts:
    87→            dir_profiles[dir_key]["zones"] = dict(zone_counts)
    88→        if imports_from:
    89→            dir_profiles[dir_key]["imports_from_dirs"] = dict(
    90→                imports_from.most_common(10)
    91→            )
    92→        if imported_by:
    93→            dir_profiles[dir_key]["imported_by_dirs"] = dict(
    94→                imported_by.most_common(10)
    95→            )
    96→
    97→    structure["directory_profiles"] = dir_profiles
    98→
    99→    root_files = []
   100→    for rel_path in dir_files.get(".", []):
   101→        info = file_info[rel_path]
   102→        role = "core" if info["fan_in"] >= 5 else "peripheral"
   103→        root_files.append(
   104→            {
   105→                "file": rel_path,
   106→                "loc": info["loc"],
   107→                "fan_in": info["fan_in"],
   108→                "fan_out": info["fan_out"],
   109→                "role": role,
   110→            }
   111→        )
   112→    if root_files:
   113→        root_files.sort(key=lambda item: -item["fan_in"])
   114→        structure["root_files"] = root_files
   115→
   116→    edge_counts: Counter = Counter()
   117→    for dir_key, profile in dir_profiles.items():
   118→        for target, count in profile.get("imports_from_dirs", {}).items():
   119→            edge_counts[f"{dir_key} → {target}"] += count
   120→    if edge_counts:
   121→        structure["coupling_matrix"] = dict(edge_counts.most_common(20))
   122→
   123→    return structure
   124→
   125→
   126→__all__ = ["compute_structure_context"]
   127→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — front-inject never-scored (placeholder)
     6→  dimensions when objective backlog is manageable; otherwise interleave them on
     7→  a bounded cadence.
     8→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — inject stale (previously-scored) dimensions with
     9→  bounded interleaving so stale refresh work cannot starve behind objective
    10→  backlog forever.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    16→
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    19→
    20→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    21→[REDACTED]
    22→_UNSCORED_MARKER = "last_unscored_injected_scan"
    23→_STALE_MARKER = "last_stale_injected_scan"
    24→
    25→# Guardrails to avoid queue inversion while still preventing starvation.
    26→_UNSCORED_FRONT_INJECT_OBJECTIVE_MAX = 40
    27→_UNSCORED_MAX_PER_SCAN = 2
    28→_UNSCORED_INTERLEAVE_EVERY_SCANS = 2
    29→_UNSCORED_INTERLEAVE_INDEX = 12
    30→
    31→_STALE_MAX_PER_SCAN = 2
    32→_STALE_INTERLEAVE_EVERY_SCANS = 3
    33→_STALE_INTERLEAVE_INDEX = 24
    34→
    35→
    36→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    37→# Result dataclasses
    38→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    39→
    40→@dataclass
    41→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    42→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    43→
    44→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    46→
    47→    @property
    48→    def changes(self) -> int:
    49→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    50→
    51→
    52→@dataclass
    53→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    54→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    55→
    56→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    57→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    58→
    59→    @property
    60→    def changes(self) -> int:
    61→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    62→
    63→
    64→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    65→# ID helpers
    66→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    67→
    68→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    69→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    70→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    71→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    72→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    73→    )
    74→
    75→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    76→    if not dim_scores:
    77→        return set()
    78→
    79→    stale: set[str] = set()
    80→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    81→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    82→            continue
    83→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    84→        if dim_key:
    85→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    86→    return stale
    87→
    88→
    89→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    90→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    91→
    92→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    93→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    94→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    97→
    98→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    99→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
   100→        return set()

    39→def build_holistic_context(
    40→    path: Path,
    41→    lang: object,
    42→    state: dict,
    43→    files: list[str] | None = None,
    44→) -> dict[str, object]:
    45→    """Gather codebase-wide data for holistic review."""
    46→    return build_holistic_context_model(path, lang, state, files=files).to_dict()
    47→
    48→
    49→def build_holistic_context_model(
    50→    path: Path,
    51→    lang,
    52→    state: dict,
    53→    files: list[str] | None = None,
    54→) -> HolisticContext:
    55→    """Gather holistic context and return a typed context contract."""
    56→    selected_files = select_holistic_files(path, lang, files)
    57→
    58→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
    59→    if not already_cached:
    60→        enable_file_cache()
    61→    try:
    62→        return _build_holistic_context_inner(path, selected_files, lang, state)
    63→    finally:
    64→        if not already_cached:
    65→            disable_file_cache()
    66→
    67→
    68→def _build_holistic_context_inner(
    69→    path: Path, files: list[str], lang, state: dict
    70→) -> HolisticContext:
    71→    """Inner holistic context builder (runs with file cache enabled)."""
    72→    file_contents = _read_file_contents(files)
    73→    allowed_rel_files = {
    74→        rel(filepath)
    75→        for filepath in files
    76→        if isinstance(filepath, str) and filepath
    77→    }
    78→
    79→    context = HolisticContext(
    80→        architecture=_architecture_context(lang, file_contents),
    81→        coupling=_coupling_context(file_contents),
    82→        conventions={
    83→            "naming_by_directory": _naming_conventions_context(file_contents),
    84→            "sibling_behavior": _sibling_behavior_context(file_contents, base_path=path),
    85→        },
    86→        errors={
    87→            "strategy_by_directory": _error_strategy_context(file_contents),
    88→        },
    89→        abstractions=_abstractions_context(file_contents),
    90→        dependencies=_dependencies_context(state, allowed_files=allowed_rel_files),
    91→        testing=_testing_context(
    92→            lang,
    93→            state,
    94→            file_contents,
    95→            allowed_files=allowed_rel_files,
    96→        ),
    97→        api_surface=_api_surface_context(lang, file_contents),
    98→        structure=compute_structure_context(file_contents, lang),
    99→    )
   100→
   101→    auth_ctx = gather_auth_context(file_contents, rel_fn=rel)
   102→    if auth_ctx:
   103→        context.authorization = auth_ctx
   104→
   105→    ai_debt = gather_ai_debt_signals(file_contents, rel_fn=rel)
   106→    if ai_debt.get("file_signals"):
   107→        context.ai_debt_signals = ai_debt
   108→
   109→    migration = gather_migration_signals(file_contents, lang, rel_fn=rel)
   110→    if migration:
   111→        context.migration_signals = migration
   112→
   113→    context.codebase_stats = _codebase_stats(file_contents)
   114→
   115→    # Enrich with aggregated mechanical detector evidence.
   116→    evidence = gather_mechanical_evidence(state, allowed_files=allowed_rel_files)
   117→    if evidence:
   118→        context.scan_evidence = evidence
   119→        _enrich_sections_from_evidence(context, evidence)
   120→
   121→    context.normalize_sections(strict=True)
   122→    return context
   123→
   124→
   125→def _enrich_sections_from_evidence(
   126→    context: HolisticContext, evidence: dict
   127→) -> None:
   128→    """Merge mechanical evidence into existing holistic context sections."""
   129→    if "complexity_hotspots" in evidence:
   130→        context.abstractions["complexity_hotspots"] = evidence["complexity_hotspots"]
   131→    if "error_hotspots" in evidence:
   132→        context.errors["exception_hotspots"] = evidence["error_hotspots"]
   133→    if "mutable_globals" in evidence:
   134→        context.errors["mutable_globals"] = evidence["mutable_globals"]
   135→    if "boundary_violations" in evidence:
   136→        context.coupling["boundary_violations"] = evidence["boundary_violations"]
   137→    if "deferred_import_density" in evidence:
   138→        context.dependencies["deferred_import_density"] = evidence["deferred_import_density"]
   139→    if "duplicate_clusters" in evidence:
   140→        context.conventions["duplicate_clusters"] = evidence["duplicate_clusters"]
   141→    if "naming_drift" in evidence:
   142→        context.conventions["naming_drift"] = evidence["naming_drift"]
   143→    if "flat_dir_findings" in evidence:
   144→        context.structure["flat_dir_findings"] = evidence["flat_dir_findings"]
   145→

1→"""Path resolution and exclusion matching helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import tempfile
     7→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_project_root
    11→
    12→
    13→def matches_exclusion(rel_path: str, exclusion: str) -> bool:
    14→    """Check if a relative path matches an exclusion pattern."""
    15→    parts = Path(rel_path).parts
    16→    if exclusion in parts:
    17→        return True
    18→    if "*" in exclusion:
    19→        if any(fnmatch(part, exclusion) for part in parts):
    20→            return True
    21→        # Full-path glob match for patterns with directory separators
    22→        # (e.g. "Wan2GP/**" should match "Wan2GP/models/rf.py").
    23→        if "/" in exclusion or os.sep in exclusion:
    24→            normalized_path = rel_path.lstrip("./")
    25→            if fnmatch(normalized_path, exclusion):
    26→                return True
    27→    if "/" in exclusion or os.sep in exclusion:
    28→        normalized = exclusion.rstrip("/").rstrip(os.sep)
    29→        return rel_path.startswith(normalized + "/") or rel_path.startswith(
    30→            normalized + os.sep
    31→        )
    32→    return False
    33→
    34→
    35→def normalize_path_separators(path: str) -> str:
    36→    return path.replace("\\", "/")
    37→
    38→
    39→def safe_relpath(path: str | Path, start: str | Path) -> str:
    40→    try:
    41→        return os.path.relpath(str(path), str(start))
    42→    except ValueError:
    43→        return str(Path(path).resolve())
    44→
    45→
    46→def rel(path: str) -> str:
    47→    root = get_project_root()
    48→    resolved = Path(path).resolve()
    49→    try:
    50→        return normalize_path_separators(str(resolved.relative_to(root)))
    51→    except ValueError:
    52→        return normalize_path_separators(safe_relpath(resolved, root))
    53→
    54→
    55→def resolve_path(filepath: str) -> str:
    56→    """Resolve a filepath to absolute, handling both relative and absolute."""
    57→    p = Path(filepath)
    58→    if p.is_absolute():
    59→        return str(p.resolve())
    60→    return str((get_project_root() / filepath).resolve())
    61→
    62→
    63→def resolve_scan_file(
    64→    filepath: str | Path,
    65→    *,
    66→    scan_root: str | Path | None = None,
    67→) -> Path:
    68→    """Resolve a scan file path with explicit scan-root-first semantics.
    69→
    70→    Relative file paths are resolved against ``scan_root`` first (when provided)
    71→    and then against the process project root as a fallback.
    72→    """
    73→    p = Path(filepath)
    74→    if p.is_absolute():
    75→        return p.resolve()
    76→
    77→    root = get_project_root()
    78→    if scan_root is not None:
    79→        scan_root_path = Path(scan_root)
    80→        scan_root_abs = (
    81→            scan_root_path.resolve()
    82→            if scan_root_path.is_absolute()
    83→            else (root / scan_root_path).resolve()
    84→        )
    85→        scan_candidate = (scan_root_abs / p).resolve()
    86→        if scan_candidate.exists():
    87→            return scan_candidate
    88→
    89→    return (root / p).resolve()
    90→
    91→
    92→def safe_write_text(filepath: str | Path, content: str) -> None:
    93→    """Atomically write text to a file using temp+rename."""
    94→    p = Path(filepath)
    95→    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    96→    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=p.parent, suffix=".tmp")
    97→    try:
    98→        with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as f:
    99→            f.write(content)
   100→        os.replace(tmp, str(p))
   101→    except OSError:
   102→        if os.path.exists(tmp):
   103→            os.unlink(tmp)
   104→        raise
   105→
   106→
   107→__all__ = [
   108→    "matches_exclusion",
   109→    "normalize_path_separators",
   110→    "rel",
   111→    "resolve_path",
   112→    "resolve_scan_file",
   113→    "safe_relpath",
   114→    "safe_write_text",
   115→]
   116→
```

1→"""Public path and snippet helpers used by command/runtime code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Callable
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core._internal import text_utils as _text_utils
    10→
    11→
    12→class _DynamicPathProxy:
    13→    """Path-like proxy that resolves on access via runtime-root-aware accessors."""
    14→
    15→    def __init__(self, resolver: Callable[[], Path], *, label: str) -> None:
    16→        self._resolver = resolver
    17→        self._label = label
    18→
    19→    def _value(self) -> Path:
    20→        return self._resolver()
    21→
    22→    def __fspath__(self) -> str:
    23→        return str(self._value())
    24→
    25→    def __str__(self) -> str:
    26→        return str(self._value())
    27→
    28→    def __repr__(self) -> str:
    29→        return f"{self._label}({self._value()!s})"
    30→
    31→    def __truediv__(self, other: object) -> Path:
    32→        return self._value() / other
    33→
    34→    def __rtruediv__(self, other: object) -> Path:
    35→        return Path(other) / self._value()
    36→
    37→    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> object:
    38→        return getattr(self._value(), name)
    39→
    40→
    41→# Backwards-compatible path exports that now honor runtime-root accessors.
    42→PROJECT_ROOT = _text_utils.PROJECT_ROOT
    43→DEFAULT_PATH = _DynamicPathProxy(
    44→    lambda: get_project_root() / "src",
    45→    label="DEFAULT_PATH",
    46→)
    47→SRC_PATH = _DynamicPathProxy(
    48→    lambda: get_project_root() / os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_SRC", "src"),
    49→    label="SRC_PATH",
    50→)
    51→
    52→
    53→def get_project_root() -> Path:
    54→    """Return the active runtime project root."""
    55→    return _text_utils.get_project_root()
    56→
    57→
    58→def get_default_path() -> Path:

    60→    return get_project_root() / "src"
    61→
    62→
    63→def get_src_path() -> Path:
    64→    """Return configured source path from runtime root."""
    65→    return get_project_root() / os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_SRC", "src")
    66→
    67→
    68→def read_code_snippet(filepath: str, line: int, context: int = 1) -> str | None:
    69→    """Read a snippet around a 1-based line number."""
    70→    return _text_utils.read_code_snippet(
    71→        filepath,
    72→        line,
    73→        context,
    74→        project_root=get_project_root(),
    75→    )
    76→
    77→
    78→def matches_exclusion(rel_path: str, exclusion: str) -> bool:
    79→    """Check if a relative path matches an exclusion pattern."""
    80→    from desloppify.core import file_paths as file_paths_mod
    81→
    82→    return file_paths_mod.matches_exclusion(rel_path, exclusion)
    83→
    84→
    85→def normalize_path_separators(path: str) -> str:
    86→    """Normalize path separators to forward slashes for stable display."""
    87→    from desloppify.core import file_paths as file_paths_mod
    88→
    89→    return file_paths_mod.normalize_path_separators(path)
    90→
    91→
    92→def safe_relpath(path: str | Path, start: str | Path) -> str:
    93→    """Best-effort relpath helper that tolerates drive boundary mismatches."""
    94→    from desloppify.core import file_paths as file_paths_mod
    95→
    96→    return file_paths_mod.safe_relpath(path, start)
    97→
    98→
    99→def rel(path: str) -> str:
   100→    """Return project-root-relative path using normalized separators."""
   101→    from desloppify.core import file_paths as file_paths_mod
   102→
   103→    return file_paths_mod.rel(path)
   104→
   105→
   106→def resolve_path(filepath: str) -> str:
   107→    """Resolve filepath to an absolute path from the active project root."""
   108→    from desloppify.core import file_paths as file_paths_mod
   109→
   110→    return file_paths_mod.resolve_path(filepath)
   111→
   112→
   113→def resolve_scan_file(

   120→
   121→    return file_paths_mod.resolve_scan_file(filepath, scan_root=scan_root)

    90→def _print_review_quality(quality: object, *, colorize_fn) -> None:
    91→    """Render merged review quality summary when present."""

    94→    coverage = quality.get("dimension_coverage")

    98→    )
    99→    if not isinstance(high_missing_issue_note, int | float):
   100→        high_missing_issue_note = quality.get(

   102→        )
   103→    finding_pressure = quality.get("finding_pressure")

   105→    if not isinstance(coverage, int | float) or not isinstance(density, int | float):

   108→    pressure_segment = ""
   109→    if isinstance(finding_pressure, int | float) and isinstance(dims_with_findings, int):
   110→        pressure_segment = (

   112→            f"across {dims_with_findings} dims"
   113→        )
   114→    print(

   120→            f"{pressure_segment}",
