```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
   296→__all__ = [
   297→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   298→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   299→    "build_subjective_items",
   300→    "detail_dict",
   301→    "is_review_finding",
   302→    "is_subjective_finding",
   303→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   304→    "review_finding_weight",
   305→    "scope_matches",
   306→    "slugify",
   307→    "status_matches",
   308→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   309→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   310→]
   311→
```

```
1→"""Finding prioritization/selection helpers for next items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanItem, PlanState
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
     7→    QueueBuildOptions,
     8→    build_work_queue,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→def get_next_items(
    13→    state: PlanState,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int = 1,
    16→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    17→) -> list[PlanItem]:
    18→    """Get the N highest-priority open findings.
    19→
    20→    Legacy plan API intentionally returns only finding items (not synthetic
    21→    subjective queue items) so existing planner consumers stay stable.
    22→    """
    23→    result = build_work_queue(
    24→        state,
    25→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    26→            tier=tier,
    27→            count=count,
    28→            scan_path=scan_path,
    29→            status="open",
    30→            include_subjective=False,
    31→            no_tier_fallback=True,
    32→        ),
    33→    )
    34→    return [item for item in result["items"] if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    35→
    36→
    37→def get_next_item(
    38→    state: PlanState,
    39→    tier: int | None = None,
    40→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    41→) -> PlanItem | None:
    42→    """Get the highest-priority open finding."""
    43→    items = get_next_items(state, tier=tier, count=1, scan_path=scan_path)
    44→    return items[0] if items else None
    45→
```

```
1→"""Finding generation pipeline (phase execution and normalization)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import is_subjective_phase
    10→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap
    11→from desloppify.core.file_paths import rel
    12→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_project_root
    13→from desloppify.languages import auto_detect_lang, available_langs, get_lang
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, LangConfig
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun, make_lang_run
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import Finding
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class PlanScanOptions:
    22→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    23→
    24→    include_slow: bool = True
    25→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    26→    profile: str = "full"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    30→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_lang(
    34→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    35→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    36→    if lang is not None:
    37→        return lang
    38→
    39→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    40→    if detected is None:
    41→        langs = available_langs()
    42→        if not langs:
    43→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    44→        detected = langs[0]
    45→    return get_lang(detected)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    49→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    53→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    54→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    55→    )
    56→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    57→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    58→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    59→    )
    60→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    61→
    62→    from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import capability_report
    63→
    64→    report = capability_report(lang)
    65→    if report is not None:
    66→        present, missing = report
    67→        if present:
    68→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    69→        if missing:
    70→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    74→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    75→    phases = lang.phases
    76→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    77→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    78→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    79→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    80→    return phases
    81→
    82→
    83→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    84→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    85→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    86→
    87→    total = len(phases)
    88→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    89→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    90→        phase_findings, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    91→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    92→        findings.extend(phase_findings)
    93→
    94→    return findings, all_potentials
    95→
    96→
    97→def _stamp_finding_context(findings: list[Finding], lang: LangRun) -> None:
    98→    if not findings:
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    zone_policies = None
   102→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   103→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   104→
   105→    for finding in findings:
   106→        finding["lang"] = lang.name
   107→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   108→            continue
   109→
   110→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(finding.get("file", ""))
   111→        finding["zone"] = zone.value
   112→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   113→        if policy and finding.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   114→            finding["confidence"] = "low"
   115→
   116→
   117→def _generate_findings_from_lang(
   118→    path: Path,
   119→    lang: LangRun,
   120→    *,
   121→    include_slow: bool = True,
   122→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   123→    profile: str = "full",
   124→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   125→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   126→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   127→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   128→    findings, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   129→    _stamp_finding_context(findings, lang)
   130→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(findings)} findings")
   131→    return findings, all_potentials
   132→
   133→
   134→def generate_findings(
   135→    path: Path,
   136→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   137→    *,
   138→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   139→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   140→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized findings."""
   141→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   142→
   143→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, get_project_root())
   144→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   145→    return _generate_findings_from_lang(
   146→        path,
   147→        runtime_lang,
   148→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   149→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   150→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
   151→    )
   152→
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_unscored_ids,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    17→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    18→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    19→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    20→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    21→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    22→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Grouping key computation
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    30→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    31→
    32→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    33→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    34→    """
    35→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    36→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    37→    if kind:
    38→        return kind
    39→
    40→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    41→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    42→    parts = fid.split("::")
    43→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    44→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    45→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    46→        candidate = parts[-1]
    47→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    48→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    49→            return candidate
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    54→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    55→
    56→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    57→    """
    58→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→
    60→    if meta is None:
    61→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    62→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    63→
    64→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    65→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    66→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    67→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    68→        if dimension:
    69→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    70→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    71→
    72→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    73→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    74→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    75→    ):
    76→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    77→        if file:
    78→            normalized = str(file).strip().replace("\\", "/")
    79→            if normalized:
    80→                return f"file::{detector}::{normalized}"
    81→
    82→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    83→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    84→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    85→        if subtype:
    86→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    87→
    88→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    89→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    90→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    91→
    92→    # Everything else → by detector
    93→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    94→
    95→
    96→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    97→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    98→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    99→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
   100→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   101→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   102→    # detector::security → auto/security
   103→    parts = key.split("::")
   104→    if len(parts) == 2:
   105→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   106→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   107→        if prefix_type == "review":
   108→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   109→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   110→    if len(parts) == 3:
   111→        if parts[0] == "file":
   112→            safe_path = parts[2].replace("/", "__").replace("\\", "__")
   113→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{safe_path}"
   114→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   115→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   116→
   117→
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→# Description and action generation
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→def _generate_description(
   123→    cluster_name: str,
   124→    members: list[dict],
   125→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   126→    subtype: str | None,
   127→) -> str:
   128→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   129→    count = len(members)
   130→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   131→
   132→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   133→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   134→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   135→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   136→
   137→    if detector == "structural":
   138→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   139→        if len(files) == 1:
   140→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   141→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   142→
   143→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   144→    if subtype:
   145→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   146→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   147→
   148→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   149→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   150→
   151→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   152→
   153→
   154→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   155→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   156→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   157→        return None
   158→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   159→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   160→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   161→        return fixer_name
   162→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   163→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   164→        if subtype in fixer:
   165→            return fixer
   166→    return None
   167→
   168→
   169→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   170→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   171→
   172→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   173→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   174→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   175→    """
   176→    if " — " in guidance:
   177→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   178→    return guidance
   179→
   180→
   181→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   182→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   183→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   184→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   185→}
   186→
   187→
   188→def _generate_action(
   189→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   190→    subtype: str | None,
   191→) -> str:
   192→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   193→
   194→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   195→    """
   196→    if meta is None:
   197→        return "review and fix each finding"
   198→
   199→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   200→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   201→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   202→        if matched_fixer:
   203→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   204→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   205→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   206→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   207→
   208→    if meta.tool == "move":
   209→        return "desloppify move"
   210→
   211→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   212→    if meta.guidance:
   213→        if subtype:
   214→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   215→        return meta.guidance
   216→
   217→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   218→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   219→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   220→    )
   221→
   222→
   223→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   224→# Main algorithm
   225→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   226→
   227→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   228→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   229→
   230→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   231→    """
   232→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   233→    changes = 0
   234→
   235→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   236→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   237→
   238→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   239→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   240→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   241→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   242→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   243→
   244→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   245→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   246→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   247→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   248→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   249→            continue
   250→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   251→            continue
   252→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   253→            continue
   254→
   255→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   256→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   257→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   258→        if key is None:
   259→            continue
   260→
   261→        groups[key].append(fid)
   262→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   263→
   264→    # Drop singleton groups
   265→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   266→
   267→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   268→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   269→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   270→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   271→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   272→            if ck:
   273→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   274→
   275→    now = utc_now()
   276→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   277→
   278→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   279→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   280→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   281→
   282→        # Representative finding for metadata
   283→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   284→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   285→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   286→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   287→
   288→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   289→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   290→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   291→
   292→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   293→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   294→
   295→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   296→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   297→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   298→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   299→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   300→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   301→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   302→                if new_ids:
   303→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   304→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   305→                    changes += 1
   306→            else:
   307→                # Replace membership wholesale
   308→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   309→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   310→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   311→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   312→                    cluster["description"] = description
   313→                    cluster["action"] = action
   314→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   315→                    changes += 1
   316→        else:
   317→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   318→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   319→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   320→                # Append a disambiguator
   321→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   322→
   323→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   324→                "name": cluster_name,
   325→                "description": description,
   326→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   327→                "created_at": now,
   328→                "updated_at": now,
   329→                "auto": True,
   330→                "cluster_key": key,
   331→                "action": action,
   332→                "user_modified": False,
   333→            }
   334→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   335→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   336→            changes += 1
   337→
   338→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   339→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   340→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   341→        for fid in member_ids:
   342→            if fid not in overrides:
   343→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   344→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   345→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   346→
   347→    # --- Subjective dimension clusters (unscored + stale) -------------------
   348→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   349→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   350→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   351→    )
   352→    unscored_state_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   353→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   354→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   355→    )
   356→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   357→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids
   358→    )
   359→
   360→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   361→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   362→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   363→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   364→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   365→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   366→
   367→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   368→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   369→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   370→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   371→            new_ids = set(unscored_queue_ids)
   372→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   373→                cluster["finding_ids"] = unscored_queue_ids
   374→                cluster["description"] = description
   375→                cluster["action"] = action
   376→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   377→                changes += 1
   378→        else:
   379→            clusters[_UNSCORED_NAME] = {
   380→                "name": _UNSCORED_NAME,
   381→                "description": description,
   382→                "finding_ids": unscored_queue_ids,
   383→                "created_at": now,
   384→                "updated_at": now,
   385→                "auto": True,
   386→                "cluster_key": _UNSCORED_KEY,
   387→                "action": action,
   388→                "user_modified": False,
   389→            }
   390→            existing_by_key[_UNSCORED_KEY] = _UNSCORED_NAME
   391→            changes += 1
   392→
   393→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   394→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY, _UNSCORED_NAME)
   395→        for fid in unscored_queue_ids:
   396→            if fid not in overrides:
   397→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   398→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   399→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   400→
   401→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   402→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   403→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   404→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   405→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   406→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   407→
   408→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   409→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   410→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   411→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   412→            new_ids = set(stale_queue_ids)
   413→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   414→                cluster["finding_ids"] = stale_queue_ids
   415→                cluster["description"] = description
   416→                cluster["action"] = action
   417→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   418→                changes += 1
   419→        else:
   420→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   421→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   422→                "description": description,
   423→                "finding_ids": stale_queue_ids,
   424→                "created_at": now,
   425→                "updated_at": now,
   426→                "auto": True,
   427→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   428→                "action": action,
   429→                "user_modified": False,
   430→            }
   431→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   432→            changes += 1
   433→
   434→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   435→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   436→        for fid in stale_queue_ids:
   437→            if fid not in overrides:
   438→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   439→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   440→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   441→
   442→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   443→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   444→        cluster = clusters[name]
   445→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   446→            continue
   447→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   448→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   449→            continue
   450→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   451→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   452→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   453→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   454→            if alive:
   455→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   456→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   457→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   458→                    changes += 1
   459→                continue
   460→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   461→        # Delete stale cluster
   462→        del clusters[name]
   463→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   464→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   465→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   466→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   467→                override["cluster"] = None
   468→                override["updated_at"] = now
   469→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   470→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   471→        changes += 1
   472→
   473→    plan["updated"] = now
   474→    return changes
   475→
   476→
   477→__all__ = [
   478→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   479→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   480→]
   481→
```

```
1→"""Canonical enums for finding attributes.
     2→
     3→StrEnum values compare equal to their string values (Confidence.HIGH == "high"),
     4→so existing code using raw strings continues to work during gradual migration.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import enum
    10→
    11→
    12→class Confidence(enum.StrEnum):
    13→    HIGH = "high"
    14→    MEDIUM = "medium"
    15→    LOW = "low"
    16→
    17→
    18→class Status(enum.StrEnum):
    19→    OPEN = "open"
    20→    FIXED = "fixed"
    21→    WONTFIX = "wontfix"
    22→    FALSE_POSITIVE = "false_positive"
    23→    AUTO_RESOLVED = "auto_resolved"
    24→    RESOLVED = "resolved"  # Legacy on-disk value; migrated to FIXED on load.
    25→
    26→
    27→_FINDING_STATUS_ORDER = (
    28→    Status.OPEN.value,
    29→    Status.FIXED.value,
    30→    Status.WONTFIX.value,
    31→    Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    32→    Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    33→)
    34→_CANONICAL_FINDING_STATUSES = frozenset(_FINDING_STATUS_ORDER)
    35→_RESOLVED_FINDING_STATUSES = frozenset(
    36→    {
    37→        Status.FIXED.value,
    38→        Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    39→        Status.WONTFIX.value,
    40→        Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    41→    }
    42→)
    43→_LEGACY_STATUS_ALIASES = {
    44→    Status.RESOLVED.value: Status.FIXED.value,
    45→}
    46→
    47→
    48→class Tier(enum.IntEnum):
    49→    AUTO_FIX = 1
    50→    QUICK_FIX = 2
    51→    JUDGMENT = 3
    52→    MAJOR_REFACTOR = 4
    53→
    54→
    55→def canonical_finding_status(value: object, *, default: str = Status.OPEN.value) -> str:
    56→    """Normalize legacy/unknown finding status values to a canonical token."""
    57→    token = str(value).strip().lower()
    58→    [REDACTED](token, token)
    59→    return token if token in _CANONICAL_FINDING_STATUSES else default
    60→
    61→
    62→def finding_status_tokens(*, include_all: bool = False) -> frozenset[str]:
    63→    """Return canonical finding-status tokens, optionally including `all`."""
    64→    if include_all:
    65→        return frozenset({*_CANONICAL_FINDING_STATUSES, "all"})
    66→    return _CANONICAL_FINDING_STATUSES
    67→
    68→
    69→def finding_status_order(*, include_all: bool = False) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    70→    """Return canonical finding status order for CLI/display flows."""
    71→    if include_all:
    72→        return (*_FINDING_STATUS_ORDER, "all")
    73→    return _FINDING_STATUS_ORDER
    74→
    75→
    76→def resolved_finding_status_tokens() -> frozenset[str]:
    77→    """Return canonical finding statuses that represent resolved states."""
    78→    return _RESOLVED_FINDING_STATUSES
    79→
    80→
    81→__all__ = [
    82→    "Confidence",
    83→    "Status",

    85→    "canonical_finding_status",
    86→    "finding_status_order",
    87→    "finding_status_tokens",
    88→    "resolved_finding_status_tokens",
    89→]
```

```
1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    41→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    42→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    43→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    44→
    45→
    46→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    47→    original_id: Required[str]
    48→    original_detector: str
    49→    original_file: str
    50→    original_summary: str
    51→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    52→    superseded_at: str
    53→    remapped_to: str | None
    54→    candidates: list[str]
    55→    note: str | None
    56→
    57→
    58→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    59→    version: Required[int]
    60→    created: Required[str]
    61→    updated: Required[str]
    62→    queue_order: list[str]
    63→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    64→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    65→    active_cluster: str | None
    66→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    67→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]

    69→    queue_sync_markers: dict[str, int]
    70→
    71→
    72→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    73→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    74→    now = utc_now()
    75→    return {
    76→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    77→        "created": now,
    78→        "updated": now,
    79→        "queue_order": [],
    80→        "deferred": [],
    81→        "skipped": {},
    82→        "active_cluster": None,
    83→        "overrides": {},
    84→        "clusters": {},
    85→        "superseded": {},
    86→        "queue_sync_markers": {},
    87→    }
    88→
    89→
    90→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    91→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    92→
    93→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    94→    """
    95→    defaults = empty_plan()
    96→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    97→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    98→
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   100→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   102→        plan["deferred"] = []
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   104→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   105→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   106→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   107→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   108→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   109→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   110→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   111→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_sync_markers"), dict):
   112→        plan["queue_sync_markers"] = {}
   113→
   114→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   115→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   116→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   117→    if deferred:
   118→        now = utc_now()
   119→        for fid in list(deferred):
   120→            if fid not in skipped:
   121→                skipped[fid] = {
   122→                    "finding_id": fid,
   123→                    "kind": "temporary",
   124→                    "reason": None,
   125→                    "note": None,
   126→                    "attestation": None,
   127→                    "created_at": now,
   128→                    "review_after": None,
   129→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   130→                }
   131→        deferred.clear()
   132→
   133→    # Normalize cluster fields
   134→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   135→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   136→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   137→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   138→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   139→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   140→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   141→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   142→
   143→
   144→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   145→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   146→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   147→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   148→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   149→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   150→
   151→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   152→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   153→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   154→    if overlap:
   155→        raise ValueError(
   156→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   157→        )
   158→
   159→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   160→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   161→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   162→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   163→            raise ValueError(
   164→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   165→            )
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = [
   169→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   170→    "Cluster",
   171→    "ItemOverride",
   172→    "PlanModel",
   173→    "SkipEntry",
   174→    "SupersededEntry",
   175→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   176→    "empty_plan",
   177→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   178→    "validate_plan",
   179→]
   180→
```

```
1→"""Shared plan constants and helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→TIER_LABELS = {
     6→    1: "Highest priority (auto-fixable + high-confidence holistic review)",
     7→    2: "Quick fixes (unused vars, dead exports, exact dupes, orphaned files, cross-tool imports)",
     8→    3: "Needs judgment (smells, near-dupes, single-use, small cycles, state sync)",
     9→    4: "Major refactors (structural decomposition, large import cycles)",
    10→}
    11→
    12→CONFIDENCE_ORDER = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
    13→
    14→
    15→def is_subjective_phase(phase) -> bool:
    16→    """Return True when a phase is subjective/review oriented."""
    17→    label = (phase.label or "").lower()
    18→    run_name = getattr(phase.run, "__name__", "").lower()
    19→    return (
    20→        "subjective" in label
    21→        or "review" in label
    22→        or run_name == "phase_subjective_review"
    23→    )
    24→
```

```
   326→    name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment

   331→
   332→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
   333→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
   334→    globals()["_on_register_callbacks"] = (*_on_register_callbacks, callback)
   335→
   336→
   337→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   338→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   339→    DETECTORS[meta.name] = meta
   340→    if meta.name not in _DISPLAY_ORDER:
   341→        _DISPLAY_ORDER.append(meta.name)
   342→    globals()["JUDGMENT_DETECTORS"] = frozenset(
   343→        name for name, m in DETECTORS.items() if m.needs_judgment
   344→    )
   345→    for cb in _on_register_callbacks:
   346→        cb()
   347→
   348→
   349→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   350→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   351→    return sorted(DETECTORS.keys())
   352→
   353→
   354→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   355→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   356→    return list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   357→
   358→
   359→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   360→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   361→    "auto_fix": "fix",
   362→    "reorganize": "move",
   363→    "refactor": "refactor",
   364→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   365→}
   366→
   367→
   368→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   369→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   370→    best = "manual"
   371→    best_pri = 99
   372→    for d in DETECTORS.values():
   373→        if d.dimension == dim_name:
   374→            pri = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(d.action_type, 99)
   375→            if pri < best_pri:
   376→                best_pri = pri
   377→                best = d.action_type
   378→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   379→
   380→
   381→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   382→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   383→    result = {}
   384→    for name, d in DETECTORS.items():
   385→        entry: dict = {
   386→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   387→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   388→        }
   389→        if d.tool:
   390→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   391→        if d.guidance:
   392→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   393→        result[name] = entry
   394→    return result
   395→
```

tool_result Read
```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    empty_plan,
    10→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16→# Helpers
    17→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→
    19→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    20→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    21→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    22→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    if finding_id in order:
    24→        order.remove(finding_id)
    25→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_position(
    29→    order: list[str],
    30→    position: str,
    31→    target: str | None = None,
    32→    offset: int | None = None,
    33→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    34→) -> int:
    35→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    36→
    37→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    38→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    39→    """
    40→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    41→
    42→    if position == "top":
    43→        return 0
    44→    if position == "bottom":
    45→        return len(order)
    46→
    47→    if position == "before" and target:
    48→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    49→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    50→                return i
    51→        return 0
    52→
    53→    if position == "after" and target:
    54→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    55→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    56→                return i + 1
    57→        return len(order)
    58→
    59→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    60→        if not finding_ids:
    61→            return 0
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    64→        current_idx_clean = None
    65→        non_moving_seen = 0
    66→        for item_id in order:
    67→            if item_id == first_id:
    68→                current_idx_clean = non_moving_seen
    69→                break
    70→            if item_id not in moving:
    71→                non_moving_seen += 1
    72→        if current_idx_clean is None:
    73→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    74→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    75→        return max(0, current_idx_clean - offset)
    76→
    77→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    78→        if not finding_ids:
    79→            return len(order)
    80→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    81→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    82→        current_idx_clean = None
    83→        non_moving_seen = 0
    84→        for item_id in order:
    85→            if item_id == first_id:
    86→                current_idx_clean = non_moving_seen
    87→                break
    88→            if item_id not in moving:
    89→                non_moving_seen += 1
    90→        if current_idx_clean is None:
    91→            return len(clean_order)
    92→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx_clean + offset)
    93→
    94→    return len(order)
    95→
    96→
    97→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    98→# Move items
    99→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   100→
   101→def move_items(
   102→    plan: PlanModel,
   103→    finding_ids: list[str],
   104→    position: str,
   105→    target: str | None = None,
   106→    offset: int | None = None,
   107→) -> int:
   108→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   109→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   110→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   111→
   112→    # Remove from skipped if present
   113→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   114→    for fid in finding_ids:
   115→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   116→
   117→    # Remove from current position in order
   118→    for fid in finding_ids:
   119→        if fid in order:
   120→            order.remove(fid)
   121→
   122→    # Resolve insertion point
   123→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   124→
   125→    # Insert in original order
   126→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   127→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   128→
   129→    return len(finding_ids)
   130→
   131→
   132→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   133→# Skip / unskip
   134→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   135→
   136→def skip_items(
   137→    plan: PlanModel,
   138→    finding_ids: list[str],
   139→    *,
   140→    kind: str = "temporary",
   141→    reason: str | None = None,
   142→    note: str | None = None,
   143→    attestation: str | None = None,
   144→    review_after: int | None = None,
   145→    scan_count: int = 0,
   146→) -> int:
   147→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   148→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   149→    now = utc_now()
   150→    count = 0
   151→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   152→    for fid in finding_ids:
   153→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   154→        skipped[fid] = {
   155→            "finding_id": fid,
   156→            "kind": kind,
   157→            "reason": reason,
   158→            "note": note,
   159→            "attestation": attestation,
   160→            "created_at": now,
   161→            "review_after": review_after,
   162→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   163→        }
   164→        count += 1
   165→    return count
   166→
   167→
   168→def unskip_items(
   169→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   170→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   171→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   172→
   173→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   174→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   175→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   176→    """
   177→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   178→    count = 0
   179→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   180→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   181→    for fid in finding_ids:
   182→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   183→        if entry is not None:
   184→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   185→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   186→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   187→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   188→            count += 1
   189→    return count, need_reopen
   190→
   191→
   192→def resurface_stale_skips(
   193→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   194→) -> list[str]:
   195→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   196→
   197→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   198→    """
   199→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   200→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   201→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   202→    for fid in list(skipped):
   203→        entry = skipped[fid]
   204→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   205→            continue
   206→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   207→        if review_after is None:
   208→            continue
   209→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   210→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   211→            skipped.pop(fid)
   212→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   213→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   214→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   215→    return resurfaced
   216→
   217→
   218→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   219→# Describe / annotate
   220→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   221→
   222→def describe_finding(
   223→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   224→) -> None:
   225→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   226→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   227→    now = utc_now()
   228→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   229→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   230→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   231→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   232→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   233→
   234→
   235→def annotate_finding(
   236→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   237→) -> None:
   238→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   239→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   240→    now = utc_now()
   241→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   242→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   243→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   244→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   245→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   246→
   247→
   248→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   249→# Clusters
   250→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   251→
   252→def create_cluster(
   253→    plan: PlanModel,
   254→    name: str,
   255→    description: str | None = None,
   256→    action: str | None = None,
   257→) -> Cluster:
   258→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   259→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   260→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   261→        raise ValueError(
   262→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   263→        )
   264→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   265→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   266→    now = utc_now()
   267→    cluster: Cluster = {
   268→        "name": name,
   269→        "description": description,
   270→        "finding_ids": [],
   271→        "created_at": now,
   272→        "updated_at": now,
   273→        "auto": False,
   274→        "cluster_key": "",
   275→        "action": action,
   276→        "user_modified": False,
   277→    }
   278→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   279→    return cluster
   280→
   281→
   282→def add_to_cluster(
   283→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   284→) -> int:
   285→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   286→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   287→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   288→    if cluster is None:
   289→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   290→
   291→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   292→    count = 0
   293→    now = utc_now()
   294→    for fid in finding_ids:
   295→        if fid not in member_ids:
   296→            member_ids.append(fid)
   297→            count += 1
   298→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   299→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   300→        if fid not in overrides:
   301→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   302→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   303→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   304→
   305→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   306→    return count
   307→
   308→
   309→def remove_from_cluster(
   310→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   311→) -> int:
   312→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   313→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   314→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   315→    if cluster is None:
   316→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   317→
   318→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   319→    now = utc_now()
   320→    count = 0
   321→    for fid in finding_ids:
   322→        if fid in member_ids:
   323→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   324→            count += 1
   325→        # Clear cluster from override
   326→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   327→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   328→            override["cluster"] = None
   329→            override["updated_at"] = now
   330→
   331→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   332→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   333→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   334→
   335→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   336→    return count
   337→
   338→
   339→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   340→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   341→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   342→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   343→    if cluster is None:
   344→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   345→
   346→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   347→    now = utc_now()
   348→    for fid in orphaned:
   349→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   350→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   351→            override["cluster"] = None
   352→            override["updated_at"] = now
   353→
   354→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   355→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   356→
   357→    return orphaned
   358→
   359→
   360→def move_cluster(
   361→    plan: PlanModel,
   362→    cluster_name: str,
   363→    position: str,
   364→    target: str | None = None,
   365→    offset: int | None = None,
   366→) -> int:
   367→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   368→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   369→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   370→    if cluster is None:
   371→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   372→
   373→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   374→    if not member_ids:
   375→        return 0
   376→
   377→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   378→
   379→
   380→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   381→# Focus
   382→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   383→
   384→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   385→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   386→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   387→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   388→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   389→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   390→
   391→
   392→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   393→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   394→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   395→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   396→
   397→
   398→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   399→# Reset
   400→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   401→
   402→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   403→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   404→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   405→    plan.clear()
   406→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   407→        plan[k] = v
   408→    plan["created"] = created
   409→
   410→
   411→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   412→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   413→
   414→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   415→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   416→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   417→    """
   418→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   419→    found = 0
   420→
   421→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   422→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   423→    for fid in finding_ids:
   424→        was_present = False
   425→        if fid in order:
   426→            order.remove(fid)
   427→            was_present = True
   428→        if fid in skipped:
   429→            skipped.pop(fid)
   430→            was_present = True
   431→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   432→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   433→            if fid in ids:
   434→                ids.remove(fid)
   435→                was_present = True
   436→        if was_present:
   437→            found += 1
   438→
   439→    return found
   440→
   441→
   442→__all__ = [
   443→    "add_to_cluster",
   444→    "annotate_finding",
   445→    "clear_focus",
   446→    "create_cluster",
   447→    "delete_cluster",
   448→    "describe_finding",
   449→    "move_cluster",
   450→    "move_items",
   451→    "purge_ids",
   452→    "remove_from_cluster",
   453→    "reset_plan",
   454→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   455→    "set_focus",
   456→    "skip_items",
   457→    "unskip_items",
   458→]
   459→
```

```
1→"""Passthrough/forwarding detection: shared classification algorithm.
     2→
     3→Language-specific extraction lives in language plugins. This module provides
     4→the shared core that classifies parameters as passthrough vs direct-use.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→import re
     8→from collections.abc import Callable
     9→
    10→
    11→def classify_passthrough_tier(
    12→    passthrough_count: int,
    13→    ratio: float,
    14→    *,
    15→    has_spread: bool = False,
    16→) -> tuple[int, str] | None:
    17→    """Classify passthrough severity into (tier, confidence) or None to skip."""
    18→    rules: list[tuple[bool, tuple[int, str]]] = [
    19→        (passthrough_count >= 20 or ratio >= 0.8, (4, "high")),
    20→        (
    21→            passthrough_count >= 8 and ratio >= 0.5,
    22→            (3, "high" if ratio >= 0.7 else "medium"),
    23→        ),
    24→        (has_spread and passthrough_count >= 4, (3, "medium")),
    25→    ]
    26→    for matched, classification in rules:
    27→        if matched:
    28→            return classification
    29→    return None
    30→
    31→
    32→def classify_params(
    33→    params: list[str],
    34→    body: str,
    35→    make_pattern: Callable[[str], str],
    36→    occurrences_per_match: int = 2,
    37→) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
    38→    """Classify params as passthrough vs direct-use.
    39→
    40→    For each param, count total word-boundary occurrences vs passthrough
    41→    pattern matches. If ALL occurrences are accounted for by passthrough
    42→    patterns, it's passthrough.
    43→
    44→    Args:
    45→        params: Parameter names to classify.
    46→        body: Function/component body text.
    47→        make_pattern: Returns a regex that matches passthrough usage of a param name.
    48→        occurrences_per_match: How many \\bname\\b occurrences each passthrough match accounts for.
    49→
    50→    Returns:
    51→        (passthrough_params, direct_params)
    52→    """
    53→    passthrough = []
    54→    direct = []
    55→    for name in params:
    56→        total = len(re.findall(rf"\b{re.escape(name)}\b", body))
    57→        if total == 0:
    58→            # Unused param — not passthrough, not direct-use either.
    59→            # Count as direct (it's destructured, just unused).
    60→            direct.append(name)
    61→            continue
    62→        pt_matches = len(re.findall(make_pattern(name), body))
    63→        pt_occurrences = pt_matches * occurrences_per_match
    64→        if pt_occurrences >= total:
    65→            passthrough.append(name)
    66→        else:
    67→            direct.append(name)
    68→    return passthrough, direct
    69→
```
